# Lessons Index

Active lessons learned from transcripts about improving **Claude Code, Claude Design, and Codex** usage. Generated by `/extract-lessons`.

**How to use this file:** read this index first to scan known lessons. Drill into individual files for detail. Superseded lessons are not listed here, find them via `grep -lr "status: superseded" lessons/`.

**Two axes.** Files are filed on disk by **topic** (`lessons/<category>/`, agents, hooks, workflows…). This index groups by **tool** (`tool:` frontmatter, `claude-code` / `claude-design` / `codex`), because that is the other question you ask. The two are independent, so a Claude Design lesson about prompting lives at `lessons/prompting/…` and is listed below under **Claude Design**. There is deliberately no `design/` or `codex/` folder: a lesson can apply to more than one tool, which a folder cannot express. Lessons with no `tool:` field are Claude Code (the pre-2026-07-17 default).

---

## Active lessons

## Claude Design

### Workflows

- [After Designing an HTML Email, Ask Claude Design to Refit It for Your Auth Provider's Variables](workflows/ask-claude-design-to-refit-an-html-email-for-your-auth-provider.md): Design the email first, then ask Claude Design how to bring it into the system that actually sends it: it re-emits the HTML with that provider's template variables so you can paste it straight in.
- [Ask Claude Design for a Video First, Then Convert It Into the Slide Deck](workflows/generate-a-video-first-then-convert-it-to-slides.md): Prompting Claude Design for a slide deck directly yields static, generic slides.
- [Ask Claude Design to Generate Tweak Knobs Instead of Prompting Each Variation](workflows/ask-claude-design-to-generate-tweak-knobs-instead-of-prompting-variations.md): After the first pass renders, prompt "give me a bunch of tweaks I can play around with".
- [Bring Your Own Hero Video to Claude Design, Controlling the Loop with First and Last Frames](workflows/bring-your-own-hero-video-to-claude-design-with-matched-first-and-last-frames.md): Claude Design can't generate video, so render it elsewhere and drag the MP4 in as context.
- [Claude Design Runs an Automatic Visual Verify Pass After Every Generation](workflows/claude-design-verify-agent-auto-corrects-visually.md): After generating a deck, page, or app screen, Claude Design's agent screenshots and reviews its own output (shown as a "verify agent" status) and silently fixes specific broken elements it spots, without being asked.
- [Collect Teammate Feedback by Sharing the Claude Design Link, Not Screenshots](workflows/share-the-claude-design-link-for-non-technical-review.md): Share the design directly with reviewers so they comment on the artifact itself, instead of asking them to run the app or trading screenshots in chat.
- [Connect the Repo So Claude Design Maps Your Real Funnel, Not a Generic One](workflows/connect-the-repo-so-claude-design-maps-your-real-funnel.md): Attach your project or codebase to Claude Design and ask it to break down your funnel: it reads the actual components people click and visualises each stage, then generates conversion variants per stage.
- [Declare Variation Count and Tweak Axes Up Front, Then Derive From the Winner](workflows/declare-variation-axes-and-tweak-toggles-up-front.md): At generation time, tell Claude Design how many variations per screen, what those variations should explore, and what the tweaks panel should let you toggle.
- [Do the Thinking in Claude Chat and Hand Claude Design Only a Finished Spec](workflows/plan-in-claude-chat-then-hand-claude-design-only-the-finished-spec.md): Never brainstorm, research, or outline inside Claude Design.
- [Drop a Finished Project's Exported HTML Into a New Claude Design Project to Carry the Brand](workflows/seed-a-new-claude-design-project-with-your-exported-html-as-the-brand.md): Export a finished Claude Design project as standalone HTML, open a NEW project, drop the file in, and prompt from there: the rendered page carries far more brand signal than a written description or a design system alone.
- [Export a Claude Design Project to Figma, Canva, or PowerPoint, Not Just HTML](workflows/claude-design-exports-to-figma-canva-powerpoint.md): Claude Design's export options include Figma, Canva, and PowerPoint alongside the raw HTML/zip export.
- [Export a Single Standalone HTML File From Claude Design When You Don't Need the Design Files](workflows/claude-design-export-standalone-html-vs-full-zip.md): Claude Design's Share menu offers a standalone-HTML export as well as the full project zip; use standalone HTML for a quick handoff to a skill or Claude Code and the zip only when you actually need the underlying design-system files.
- [Feed Design Tools a Philosophy-First Brief: Strip the PRD, Then Commit to One of Three Divergent UX Directions](workflows/design-philosophy-first-strip-prd-then-pick-one-ux-direction.md): Before generating any screens in a design tool, strip the engineering detail out of the PRD down to features/jobs/user-journeys, brainstorm three divergent UX philosophies (e.g.
- [Generate UI Structure First, Inject Real Copy on a Second Pass](workflows/generate-ui-structure-first-then-inject-copy.md): Prompt layout+style first, then real copy separately, one screen per prompt; dumping the full spec and all copy at once reliably yields mid-tier designs.
- [Keep the Camera Static When Generating a Background Loop Video for Claude Design](workflows/claude-design-static-camera-for-seamless-background-loop-video.md): When prompting an AI video model for a hero/background clip meant to loop endlessly, specify that the camera does not move so the loop isn't jarring.
- [Pick High Fidelity, Not Wireframe, When You Want a Near-Final Claude Design Prototype](workflows/claude-design-high-fidelity-not-wireframe-for-usable-output.md): Claude Design's prototype step asks for Wireframe or High Fidelity; Wireframe gives a rough unstyled mock-up, High Fidelity applies your published design system directly for an almost production-ready page.
- [Pitch With a Clickable Claude Design Prototype Instead of Building the Product](workflows/pitch-with-a-clickable-claude-design-prototype-instead-of-building.md): For validating or selling a product idea, generate a clickable Claude Design wireframe you can walk someone through, and stop there rather than having Claude Code build it.
- [Port Approved Screens to a New Form Factor by Screenshotting Them into a Fresh Chat](workflows/port-approved-screens-to-a-new-form-factor-with-screenshots.md): Once a set of mobile screens is right, screenshot them, drop the images into a new design chat, and ask for a web-app version of the same core functionality.
- [Queue Every Claude Design Comment and Drawing, Then Send Them All in One Pass](workflows/batch-claude-design-comments-and-drawings-then-send-once.md): Leave inline comments and circle regions with the draw tool across the whole design, click through the queued comments to check them, then hit Send to Claude once so the model fixes the batch together.
- [Re-skin Your Proposal in the Prospect's Own Branding Before You Send It](workflows/reskin-a-proposal-in-the-prospects-brand-before-sending.md): Take a proposal you already have, point Claude Design at the prospect's company as the brand source, and have it re-emit the same document in their palette, type and style.
- [Rebuild Stale Decks, Proposals and PDFs by Uploading the Original Into Claude Design](workflows/rebuild-stale-collateral-by-uploading-the-original-into-claude-design.md): Upload the outdated artifact itself (a Canva proposal, an old deck, a research PDF) alongside your published design system and ask Claude Design to rebuild the whole thing, then finish it on the canvas.
- [Save a Finished Claude Design Project as a Template for Reuse](workflows/claude-design-save-as-template.md): Once a Claude Design project is done, use Share > 'save as template' to turn it into a reusable starting point for future projects instead of rebuilding a similar brief from scratch.
- [Sketch UI Directly on the Claude Design Canvas Instead of Describing Layout in Words](workflows/claude-design-draw-to-code.md): Use the draw tool to sketch boxes/wireframes; Claude interprets the shapes as UI components, and the tweaks panel swaps themes without re-prompting.
- [Skip Claude Design's Wireframe Stage for a Single Page: Spend It on Journeys and App Screens](workflows/skip-the-claude-design-wireframe-stage-for-a-single-page.md): A wireframe pass before a one-page landing page is measurably wasted spend; go straight to high fidelity.
- [Type the Final Copy on the Canvas Instead of Prompting Claude Design for Micro-Edits](workflows/type-the-final-copy-on-the-claude-design-canvas-instead-of-prompting.md): When the design is right but the words are not yours, edit the text in place on the design rather than spending prompt rounds asking for wording tweaks.
- [Use Claude Design's Animation Template to Edit Video, Anchored to Your Brand](workflows/claude-design-animation-template-for-branded-motion-graphics.md): From template → Animation opens a timeline-based motion-design surface that will animate an uploaded MP4 with synced captions and motion graphics.
- [Use Claude Design's Draw Tool Only for Things That Aren't Real Elements](workflows/draw-on-the-claude-design-canvas-only-for-things-that-arent-elements.md): Circling a real component to say "make this bigger" misfires, and sometimes fails to capture at all.
- [When You Don't Know How to Use an Asset, Ask Claude Design to Mock Up Placements For It](workflows/ask-claude-design-to-mock-up-placements-for-an-asset.md): After Claude Design produces an asset you like but have no plan for, ask it to show the asset in context on your site in several options, and pick the placement rather than inventing one.
- [On a Pro or Max Plan, Build the Animation in Claude Design and Use Its Comment Button to Edit One Spot](workflows/build-an-animation-in-claude-design-on-pro-or-max-when-motion-is-team-only.md): Claude Motion was Team and Enterprise only in this video, but the same prompt in Claude Design (Artifacts, then Design) produced a near-identical animation on Pro or Max, editable by commenting on a single element.

### Skills

- [Teach Claude Design a Framework by Attaching Its SKILL.md to the Project](skills/attach-a-framework-skill-markdown-to-a-claude-design-project.md): Claude Design has no skills directory, but you can download a skill's raw markdown and attach it as a project **file**, dropping in a motion-graphics skill's markdown had Design use it to write rendered animation.

### Configuration

- [Budget 10-15 Minutes and Real Quota for Claude Design's Design-System Ingestion](configuration/claude-design-design-system-ingestion-takes-10-to-15-minutes.md): Creating a Claude Design design system (brand name, repo, files, logos) takes roughly 10-15 minutes to ingest into a spec, and that ingestion alone burns a meaningful chunk of quota.
- [Import a Pre-Built Brand Kit From Get Design Into Claude Design](configuration/get-design-md-brand-kit-import.md): Get Design (getdesign.com) publishes free design.md brand-kit files for dozens of major brands; paste one into Claude Design's notes to seed a design system instantly instead of relying only on screenshots.
- [Seed a Claude Design System With a Real Brand Kit Instead of Letting Claude Pick](configuration/seed-design-system-with-a-real-brand-kit.md): Don't let Claude choose your colors and fonts.

### Context Management

- [Attach Only the Files Claude Design Needs, Never the Whole Repo](context-management/attach-only-the-files-claude-design-needs.md): Claude Design reads every file you attach to a project and bills all of it against its own weekly pool. Hand it the two or three files or screens that matter, not your project folder.
- [Export and Restart Rather Than Iterating in One Long Claude Design Thread](context-management/export-and-restart-instead-of-iterating-in-one-long-claude-design-thread.md): Claude Design bills the whole accumulated thread every turn and eventually surfaces "Start a new chat to save 110,000 tokens of context".

### Model Selection

- [Generate on Opus in Claude Design, Then Drop to Sonnet to Edit](model-selection/opus-to-generate-sonnet-to-edit-in-claude-design.md): Claude Design has its own model picker. Use Opus 4.7 for the design generation pass where quality compounds, then switch to Sonnet 4.6 for edits, which costs roughly half.

### MCP

- [Wire the Mobin Design-Reference MCP Into Claude Design via a Claude Desktop Connector](mcp/mobin-design-inspiration-mcp-in-claude-design.md): Mobin's 500k-screen MCP has no add-MCP path in the Design web UI, so connect it in Claude Desktop (Settings > Connectors > Browse > Mobin) and then ask Design to pull named screens, elements, or whole flows as generation input.

### Prompting

- [Answer Claude Design's Question Round With 'Decide for Me' to Skip Manual Choices](prompting/claude-design-decide-for-me-during-question-round.md): When Claude Design's animation/video wizard asks a batch of setup questions (talking-head placement, visual energy, motion graphics style), you can just reply 'decide for me' and it picks sensible defaults instead of you specifying each one.
- [Change One Visual Dimension per Claude Design Prompt](prompting/one-visual-change-per-claude-design-prompt.md): Unlike Claude Code, where a mega-prompt spawns a to-do list and gets worked through, Claude Design silently drops most of a multi-change request.
- [Give Every Color a Job: Neutral Majority, Accent Only Where You Want a Click](prompting/give-every-color-a-job-neutral-base-accent-on-click-targets.md): Color's AI tell is allocation, not palette: keep most of the page neutral and let bold color appear only on buttons, links, and the one word you want to pop; ban gradients outright.
- [Leave the Brief Open Enough to Trigger Claude Design's Question Round](prompting/keep-the-claude-design-brief-open-enough-to-trigger-its-question-round.md): Claude Design's clarifying questions are its planning stage, and they only fire when the brief has room. Over-prescribe and you skip the interview entirely, Claude just assumes the answers instead.
- [Name a Real Product and Era Instead of Reaching for Adjectives Like "Clean"](prompting/reference-real-products-and-state-negatives-instead-of-adjectives.md): "Make it clean" or "minimalistic" returns the training-data average. "Linear 2023 with higher density" names a target the model actually knows.
- [Pin Every Small Claude Design Edit to One Element and Name the Exact Value](prompting/pin-claude-design-edits-to-one-element-with-an-exact-value.md): Use the comment/edit/draw tools on the specific element instead of describing the change in chat, state the exact value you want, and make one change per message.
- [Rebuild a Reference Site in Your Brand by Handing Claude Design the Gallery's Prompt or URL](prompting/feed-claude-design-a-reference-gallery-url-or-its-prompt.md): Motion and component galleries publish the prompt that produced each effect.
- [Replace "Modern" With a Numeric Type and Spacing Contract in the Design Brief](prompting/numeric-type-and-spacing-contract-in-the-design-brief.md): State measurable rules instead of adjectives: one display font, body copy at 16-18px, every gap a multiple of 8, and a layout that deliberately breaks the centred column.
- [Split the Brand Source From the Structure Source in One Design Prompt](prompting/split-brand-and-structure-sources-in-one-design-prompt.md): Point Claude Design at two different references with explicitly different jobs: "use the design system for the branding, and use the attached screenshot for the page structure.".

### Gotchas

- [A Claude Design Chat Accumulates Multiple Files: Switch Between Them at the Top Left](gotchas/claude-design-file-switcher-is-at-the-top-left.md): Iterating inside one Claude Design chat often creates several separate files, not one evolving file.
- [Claude Design Can't Place Freely or Invent a Graphic: Make the Asset Elsewhere](gotchas/claude-design-cannot-place-freeform-or-invent-graphics.md): Claude Design is deterministic layout, not a canvas: you can't drag anything, and it can't conjure a custom graphic.
- [Claude Design Cannot Hear Your Video: Paste a Word-Level Timestamped Transcript](gotchas/paste-a-word-level-transcript-claude-design-cannot-hear-video.md): Claude Design can't watch or listen to an uploaded MP4. Without a transcript carrying word-level timestamps, nothing it overlays can sync to what you're actually saying.
- [Claude Design Caps Media Uploads Around 30-40MB](gotchas/claude-design-asset-upload-size-cap.md): Claude Design rejects or silently fails on long/large video uploads. The creator got 15-second clips through reliably and a 20-second clip once, but flagged a roughly 30-40MB ceiling.
- [Claude Design Direct Edits Have No Undo: Read the Current Value Before You Change It](gotchas/claude-design-direct-edits-have-no-undo.md): Only the tweaks panel has a Reset; a direct canvas edit in Claude Design cannot be undone, so note the existing value (size, color, copy) before changing it so you can restore it by hand.
- [Claude Design Has No Revert-to-Previous-Prompt Button, Unlike Lovable](gotchas/claude-design-no-prompt-history-revert.md): Claude Design has no way to roll back to an earlier prompt-driven version of a project the way Lovable does, so a bad prompt can't simply be undone by reverting.
- [Claude Design Lives at claude.ai/design, and the Desktop App Now Opens It From a Design Button](gotchas/claude-design-is-web-only.md): Claude Design is an Anthropic Labs project at claude.ai/design. It launched web-only (June 2026), but by August 2026 the Claude desktop app has a design button that takes you to it. No source here confirms the mobile app.
- [Claude Design Now Bills Your Normal Plan Usage, Not a Separate Design Quota](gotchas/claude-design-now-bills-your-normal-plan-usage-not-a-separate-quota.md): Claude Design originally metered a separate weekly design allowance that users burned through on a single website.
- [Claude Design's Comment Box Takes No Image Attachment: Upload in the Main Chat and Reference It](gotchas/claude-design-comment-box-rejects-image-attachments.md): You cannot attach an image to a pinned comment in Claude Design, which is exactly where you reach for one.
- [Claude Design's Draw-Tool Comment Box Can Hide What You're Typing](gotchas/claude-design-draw-comment-input-obscured-bug.md): When you circle something with the draw tool and start typing feedback, the comment input can get visually stuck or covered so you can't see the text you're entering, a research-preview UI bug two separate creators hit independently.
- [Claude Design's Mobile Device Frame Can Overlap Its Own Status Bar With Content: Ask It to Clean Up Formatting](gotchas/claude-design-device-frame-elements-overlap-ask-for-cleanup.md): Wireframes generated for a named device frame (e.g. iPhone) can render the phone's simulated time/battery bar crossing over real page content; explicitly ask Claude Design to clean up the formatting for that frame rather than assuming the device chrome is accurate.
- [Cut Retakes and Dead Space Yourself Before Uploading Footage to Claude Design](gotchas/cut-retakes-and-dead-space-before-uploading-video-to-claude-design.md): Claude Design cannot tell a flubbed take from a real one, so edit retakes and dead space out of raw footage by hand before uploading; steering the model cut-by-cut through prompts costs more than doing the rough cut yourself.
- [Don't Ask Claude Design to Optimize Your Copy: Use a Copywriting or CRO Skill Instead](gotchas/use-dedicated-copywriting-skill-not-claude-design-for-copy.md): Claude Design is for the visual layer; when the marketing text on a page needs work, hand that off to a dedicated copywriting or conversion-rate-optimization skill rather than iterating on wording inside Claude Design.
- [Don't Trust /clear in Claude Design to Fully Reset Context](gotchas/claude-design-slash-clear-context-clearing-unverified.md): Claude Design accepts a /clear command and replies that it's cleared, but there's no confirmation it actually purges the underlying context window rather than just the visible thread.
- [Don't Walk Away During Claude Design's Questions: They Time Out Into Defaults](gotchas/claude-design-question-round-times-out-into-defaults.md): Claude Design's clarifying questions expire if you leave them unanswered. It fills in its own defaults and builds anyway, so the run completes and quietly spends your quota on a brief you never wrote.
- [Expect Claude Design to Redraw Your Uploaded Logo: Reject It in the Design-System Review](gotchas/claude-design-redraws-uploaded-logos.md): Claude Design re-renders logos rather than placing the PNG you uploaded, mangling detailed icon marks while simple letterform wordmarks survive.
- [In a Claude Design System, the Pasted Text Kit Overrides Your Screenshots](gotchas/text-brand-kit-overrides-screenshots-in-claude-design.md): When the reference screenshots and the pasted text brand kit disagree, Claude Design follows the text: Stripe buttons came out rounded per the design.md spec even though the screenshots showed square ones.
- [Publish the Claude Design System Before Starting a Prototype, or It Won't Be Selectable](gotchas/publish-the-design-system-before-starting-a-prototype.md): An unpublished design system cannot be attached to a new prototype. Finish the review queue, publish, and confirm the system's chip appears in the prompt box before you generate anything.
- [Zoom Out in Your Browser When Claude Design's Brief Button Is Pushed Off-Screen by Attachments](gotchas/zoom-out-to-reach-create-button-claude-design.md): Attaching many reference screenshots to a Claude Design brief pushes the 'describe what you want to create' button off the visible page; zoom out in the browser to reach it rather than assuming the UI is broken.
- [Claude Design Now Opens on Every Plan, but the Free Plan Tops Out at Sonnet 5.5](gotchas/claude-design-opens-on-every-plan-free-plan-tops-out-at-sonnet.md): Claude Design came out of beta and is no longer gated behind a paid plan: a free account can open it (Artifacts, then Design) and run a prompt, but Sonnet 5.5 is the highest model there, and heavy work may not fit the free usage.

## Codex

### Workflows

- [Anchor the Pre-Launch Security Audit to a Named Standard and Make It Separate Human-Only Items](workflows/anchor-a-prelaunch-security-audit-to-a-named-standard.md): Before an agent-built app takes real users, run a read-only audit whose prompt cites an explicit standard (the OWASP LLM top-10 repo) rather than asking for 'a security check', and require the report to split agent-fixable findings from the ones only you can do.
- [Ask a Verifier Agent to Run a Two-Tenant Test for Cross-Tenant Data Leakage](workflows/two-tenant-test-for-cross-tenant-leakage.md): Before launch, have a verifier agent create two separate tenant/user accounts and specifically test whether one can see or affect the other's data.
- [Ask the Agent to Run a Name-Collision Check Before Locking In a Product Name](workflows/name-collision-check-before-locking-a-product-name.md): Before committing to a product name, have the agent search for existing products already using that name or a near-identical one.
- [Close the Last 20% of a CLI Workflow With Computer Use Instead of Abandoning the Automation](workflows/cover-the-last-mile-of-a-cli-workflow-with-computer-use.md): When a tool's CLI or API gets you most of the way but a few steps exist only in its GUI, put those steps under computer use and keep the rest scripted.
- [Fix One Element in a Long Render in the Local Studio, Not With Another Prompt Round](workflows/tweak-a-long-render-in-the-local-studio-not-another-prompt-round.md): When an agent-generated render exposes a local editor (Hyperframes serves one on localhost), nudge a single value there instead of spending another generation round on it.
- [Give the Coding Agent the Running Emulator So It Tests Its Own Build](workflows/give-the-agent-the-emulator-so-it-self-tests.md): Grant the agent access to the simulator or emulator your app runs in, so it can exercise the screen it just changed rather than handing you an untested build.
- [Leave the Commit Message Blank in Codex App to Auto-Generate One](workflows/codex-blank-commit-message-autogenerates.md): Leaving the commit message field empty in Codex app's git panel makes it auto-generate a commit message for you.
- [Open a Terminal Inside the Codex App to Call Claude Code in the Same Working Directory](workflows/open-terminal-inside-codex-to-call-claude-code.md): Codex's app has a View > Terminal option; open it to run another CLI, like Claude Code, directly in your current project folder, then hand its output back to Codex.
- [Point /goal at Unfolding Work, Then Throw the Branch Away and Rebuild From What It Learned](workflows/use-goal-for-unfolding-work-then-redo-it-from-a-distilled-spec.md): Use `/goal` for work whose solution shape you can't predict ("cut P95 checkout latency 20%") rather than pre-decomposable ticket work.
- [Point Codex's Computer Use at the App You Are Cloning, Not Just Its Docs](workflows/point-codex-computer-use-at-the-app-youre-cloning.md): When rebuilding something that already exists, have Codex drive the reference app through computer use and observe its real behaviour while it builds your version.
- [Run a Genuinely Risky Autonomous Goal on a VPS, Not on Your Own Machine](workflows/run-a-genuinely-risky-goal-run-on-a-vps-not-your-own-machine.md): An unattended multi-hour run that is allowed to experiment freely should not have your laptop as its blast radius.
- [Run Recurring Ops Work in a Notebook the Agent Treats as Its Goal, and Capture Decision Rationale Before Wrap-Up](workflows/run-recurring-ops-in-a-notebook-and-capture-decision-rationale.md): A goal cell, plan-then-approve, documented commands, and an end-of-run "why we chose this" step make each run produce the document that speeds up the next one.
- [Strip the Labels Before Asking a Second Model to Compare Two Builds](workflows/blind-the-labels-when-a-second-model-scores-two-builds.md): When you have a second agent evaluate two versions of the same thing, hand it unlabelled targets (two ports, two directories) and never say which model or approach produced which.
- [Tell Codex's Browser Agent to Try to Break the App, and Set a Scenario Floor With /goal](workflows/tell-the-codex-browser-agent-to-try-to-break-your-ui.md): Adversarial phrasing ("try your best to break it") turns Codex's browser use into a QA pass that finds data-integrity and responsiveness bugs, and /goal lets you demand a minimum number of distinct edge-case scenarios instead of accepting whatever it tried.
- [Use Codex computer use to build animations in a GUI editor that has no MCP or CLI](workflows/drive-a-gui-only-animation-editor-with-codex-computer-use.md): For an editor like Rive, tell Codex to control it with computer use and describe the new animation in plain language.
- [Voice Control in Codex Has No Screen Context - Name the Project, and Pin Your Threads](workflows/name-the-project-explicitly-when-driving-codex-by-voice.md): ChatGPT voice chat now drives Codex threads (start a task, spawn a second task in parallel, pin a thread), but it has no idea what you're looking at, so refer to work by project name; and switch the thread list from recent to pinned so thread slots stop reordering under you.
- [Watch for Codex's Colored Dot on a Chat to Know It Needs Your Attention](workflows/codex-sidebar-dot-flags-chats-needing-attention.md): Codex marks a project's chats in the sidebar with a small blue or yellow dot when that background session needs a response or has finished, so you can multitask across several chats.
- [While Reading a Stack of Diffs, Queue One Note Per Diff in the Same Codex Thread](workflows/queue-one-review-note-per-diff-in-a-single-codex-thread.md): Read a diff, write a short note, queue it, move to the next diff, and let the agent work through maybe 20 notes in one thread so all related context stays together.
- [Rename, Pin, Section and Archive Codex Threads So the Sidebar Tracks What Is Running](workflows/rename-pin-and-archive-codex-threads-to-track-long-running-work.md): Rename a thread for its high-level task, pin the active ones, group them in custom sections, and archive finished ones to keep projects and context tidy.
- [For a Hard Issue, Have Codex Spin Up Three Threads With Different Constraints, Review the PRs, Merge One](workflows/codex-spawn-competing-threads-with-different-constraints-and-merge-one.md): Ask a Codex thread to create three threads for the same issue, such as performance first, minimal diff, and simplest elegant solution (may refactor), then compare the PRs and merge the one you like.
- [Queue a Message Into Another Codex Session From a Script With codex queue --resume](workflows/codex-queue-resume-wakes-a-parked-session.md): `codex queue --thread <id|name> --message <text> --resume` drops a message into an existing session and wakes it if it is not loaded.

### Agents

- [Codex Subagents Never Auto-Invoke: Name Them Explicitly or They Don't Run](agents/codex-subagents-dont-auto-invoke.md): Codex's docs state it won't spawn a subagent unless you explicitly ask, while Claude Code decides on its own to spin up planner/explorer/reviewer agents.
- [Coordinate Codex Tasks by @-Mentioning Other Tasks Instead of Pasting Context Between Them](agents/coordinate-codex-tasks-with-at-mentions-from-the-terminal.md): CLI 0.150.0 makes tasks first-class: @-mention another task in the composer, and ask an agent to read, create, or message tasks itself.
- [Make the Agent That Found the Bugs the One That Fixes and Re-Verifies Them](agents/hand-the-bug-list-back-to-the-agent-that-found-it-as-the-fixer.md): Hand a QA agent's own findings straight back to it ('you found these, you are the orchestrator, fix the blockers and highs, then verify they are truly fixed'), so the session that was building keeps building.
- [Split a multi-stage task across Codex threads pinned to different models](agents/codex-split-a-task-across-model-pinned-threads.md): For a task with distinct stages, run separate Codex threads each pinned to a different model (a cheap cloud model for research, a local model to shortlist, the frontier model for final synthesis) and explicitly tell Codex to pass results from one thread to the next.

### Skills

- [Adapt a Claude Code Skill Before Using It in Codex: In SKILL.md, Not a Sidecar, and Front-Load Trigger Keywords](skills/codex-skills-need-sidecar-and-cap-descriptions-put-triggers-first.md): A Codex skill needs a companion sidecar/config YAML (icon, default prompt, tool dependencies, invocation policy) that Claude Code skills lack, and Codex caps how many skill descriptions it reads - so lead Codex descriptions with their trigger keywords, and keep a converter skill to port skills between the two providers.
- [Derive a Skill's Subjective Verification Steps by Asking What a Human Would Check to Approve It](skills/derive-subjective-verification-from-what-a-human-would-check.md): When a task has no hard metric (does this sound right, does this look good), ask yourself what you personally would look at before giving the output a stamp of approval, then write that exact check into the skill's verification instructions.
- [Have Your Setup-Audit Skill Write a Dated Scored Report, So You Can See the Trend](skills/write-each-setup-audit-to-a-dated-report-so-the-score-trends.md): A recurring audit of your own agent setup should write each run to its own dated file in an audits/ folder with a score, not just print to the terminal.
- [Keep Codex Skill Descriptions Short - a Long One Steals Budget From Every Other Skill](skills/keep-codex-skill-descriptions-short.md): The skill catalog renders into a fixed metadata budget; under pressure Codex shortens descriptions, then drops them, then omits whole skills, so one verbose description hides others.

### Plugins

- [Codex's plugin marketplace ships ready-made Blender/3D-asset plugins](plugins/codex-plugin-marketplace-has-3d-blender-plugins.md): Searching "Blender" in Codex's plugin marketplace turns up game-development-studio and build-3d-game-rooms.

### Settings

- [Check Codex's Rate Limits Remaining Panel for 5-Hour and Weekly Usage](settings/codex-rate-limits-remaining-panel.md): Settings > Rate Limits Remaining shows two separate percentages: how much of your rolling 5-hour session budget is left, and how much of your weekly budget is left.
- [Switch Codex's /personality Setting to Pragmatic for Concise Output](settings/codex-personality-setting-pragmatic-default.md): Codex has a /personality setting toggling between Friendly and Pragmatic; Pragmatic gives concise, task-focused, direct responses.
- [Use Codex's Pet Icon to Track Background Task Status While You Multitask](settings/codex-pet-status-indicator.md): Codex shows a small on-screen pet (chosen in Settings > Appearance > Pets) that indicates when it's actively working, letting you glance at progress without switching back to the app.

### Configuration

- [Connect Codex to a local Ollama or LM Studio model with --oss](configuration/codex-oss-flag-connects-local-ollama-model.md): Codex CLI has a built-in reserved 'ollama' model provider.
- [Move Constraints You Keep Retyping Into AGENTS.md, Repetition Is the Signal](configuration/move-repeated-prompt-constraints-into-agents-md.md): When the same constraint (framework, architecture, anything not apparent from the code) shows up in prompt after prompt, stop restating it and put it in AGENTS.md so every run inherits it.
- [Put a `## Code Review Rules` Section in AGENTS.md to Encode the Invariants Reviewers Keep Re-Explaining](configuration/code-review-rules-section-in-agents-md.md): Rule-guided review recovered 98% of required custom findings vs 58.3% baseline; each rule states a non-obvious invariant plus the safe alternative, in the AGENTS.md nearest the code.
- [Put Top-Level config.toml Keys Before Any [section] Header or Codex Silently Ignores Them](configuration/put-top-level-codex-config-keys-before-any-section-header.md): Keys like `model`, `model_context_window`, and `model_auto_compact_token_limit` must sit at the very top of `~/.codex/config.toml`, above any `[section]` header, or they are silently ignored with no error.
- [Set project_doc_fallback_filenames So Codex Reads an Existing CLAUDE.md Where No AGENTS.md Exists](configuration/set-project-doc-fallback-filenames-so-codex-reads-claude-md.md): In ~/.codex/config.toml, set project_doc_fallback_filenames = ["CLAUDE.md"] so a directory that only carries a Claude Code memory file still feeds instructions to Codex, with no rename and no duplicate file to maintain.
- [Use `AGENTS.override.md` for Temporary Codex Instructions: and Watch the 32 KiB Doc Cap](configuration/agents-override-md-and-the-project-doc-byte-cap.md): Override beats AGENTS.md at every level (one file per directory), and past `project_doc_max_bytes` instructions are dropped with no error at all.
- [Drop the instant_interrupt Override on Codex 0.163.0 and Later: Steering Mid-Response Is Now the Default](configuration/codex-steers-mid-response-by-default-drop-the-instant-interrupt-override.md): Since 0.163.0 a follow-up typed while Codex is responding steers the running turn without any opt-in, and an interrupt also stops active Code Mode cells and their nested tool calls.

### Permissions

- [A Codex Project Is a Working Directory, Not a Sandbox: It Will Wander and Reuse Other Projects' Secrets](permissions/codex-projects-are-a-working-directory-not-a-boundary.md): Codex working inside one project will still search your whole machine and silently pull artifacts from unrelated projects.
- [Answer Codex's Directory-Trust Prompt Deliberately - Trust Switches On Project-Local Hooks](permissions/codex-directory-trust-switches-on-project-local-hooks.md): Saying yes activates that repo's local config, hooks and exec policies, and trust applies to the git repository root even when you launch from a subdirectory.
- [Configure WebSocket Auth Before Exposing Codex's app-server Remotely](permissions/set-websocket-auth-before-exposing-the-codex-app-server.md): Non-loopback WebSocket listeners accept unauthenticated connections by default during rollout; pass `--ws-auth capability-token` with `--ws-token-file`.
- [Enumerate the Forbidden Actions When Computer Use Touches a Live Account](permissions/enumerate-forbidden-actions-when-computer-use-drives-a-live-account.md): Before turning computer use loose on a real signed-in account, list the actions it must not take (no messages, nothing destructive, settings only) in the prompt itself.
- [Graduate From Codex's Default Permissions to Full Access Once You Trust Your Flow](permissions/codex-auto-review-full-access-toggle-graduation.md): Codex's Settings has an Auto Review / Full Access toggle that's off by default; start on default permissions while learning a new project or skill, then flip to Full Access once you trust it.
- [Never Run Codex in Full Access Without the Sandbox and Auto Review: That Combination Is How `$HOME` Gets Deleted](permissions/never-run-codex-full-access-without-sandbox-and-auto-review.md): OpenAI's Codex lead names the exact recipe behind reported file deletions: full access + no sandbox + no auto review, where the model redefines `$HOME` for a temp dir and wipes the real one.
- [Reach for Codex's --approve-for-me Instead of Full Access on Unattended Runs](permissions/codex-approve-for-me-keeps-review-without-prompt-friction.md): It removes prompt friction while keeping the automated-review leg that full access drops, which is the leg behind reported `$HOME` deletions.

### Context Management

- [Codex App Caches Threads Across a Full App Restart](context-management/codex-app-threads-persist-across-restart.md): Codex app keeps every thread's session cached even after you fully quit and reopen the app; a bare terminal session doing the same task would be destroyed on close.
- [Raise the Codex context window to 1M with model_context_window in config.toml, and know you are overriding a tuned default](context-management/raise-the-codex-context-window-to-1m-with-model-context-window.md): The Codex lead documents the exact config.toml keys for a 1M-token window and warns that the shipped default was deliberately tuned for performance and cost, so the bigger window is a trade, not a free upgrade.
- [Select the Text First, Then "Ask in Side Chat": the Selection Becomes the Side Chat's Starting Context](context-management/select-text-then-ask-in-side-chat-to-seed-the-fork.md): Highlighting the error or diff hunk seeds the fork with that exact excerpt, instead of a bare `/side` inheriting the whole thread and guessing what you meant.
- [When Building a Custom Agent Harness on OpenAI Models, Retain Reasoning and Use Compaction, Never Rolling Truncation](context-management/retain-reasoning-and-compact-in-custom-agent-harnesses.md): Retained reasoning plus compaction took GPT-5.6 Sol from 13.3% to 38.3% on ARC-AGI-3 while cutting output tokens 6x; rolling truncation forces the model to re-derive its plan every turn.
- [Ask a Long-Running Goal for Its Status in a Side Chat, Then Steer the Fix Into the Main Thread](context-management/ask-a-running-goal-for-status-in-a-side-chat.md): To check on a goal that has run for hours without interrupting it, open a side chat (it inherits the thread's context), ask for status, and if the goal is stuck, add the fix to the main chat and steer it in.
- [Drag a Codex Thread Into a New Thread to Seed It With a Reference, Not a Full Copy](context-management/drag-one-codex-thread-onto-another-to-seed-its-context.md): In the Codex app you can drag an existing thread into another so the new one references its context and starts fresh, which differs from branching a chat (that creates a worktree).

### Model Selection

- [Check Codex's Reasoning Dial Before Every Chat: It Lands Back on Extra High](model-selection/codex-reasoning-dial-resets-to-extra-high.md): Codex splits model, reasoning (low/medium/high/extra high), and speed (standard/fast) into three independent per-chat dials, and new chats keep coming up on extra high.
- [Default Astra to Medium Effort in Codex: High and Above Bought Nothing Measurable](model-selection/default-astra-to-medium-effort-in-codex.md): Across seven controlled threads on the same research-and-build task, Astra on medium produced work on par with high, and extra high, max and ultra added time and tokens without a better result.
- [Leave Codex's Fast Speed Dial on Standard Unless You Are Genuinely Against a Clock](model-selection/leave-codex-fast-mode-off-unless-against-a-clock.md): Codex's per-chat speed dial (standard/fast) is a cost trap separate from the reasoning dial: fast buys speed you rarely need on non-time-critical work and burns your 5-hour and weekly usage windows noticeably quicker, so keep it on standard.
- [Never route a Codex subscription through a sub2api bridge: use Sign in With ChatGPT in the OSS client instead](model-selection/never-route-a-codex-subscription-through-a-sub2api-bridge-use.md): The Codex lead confirms sub2api subscription-to-API bridges trip OpenAI's fraud-prevention systems and were the common factor behind users reporting anomalous usage-limit drain, while native ChatGPT sign-in in third-party clients is explicitly sanctioned.
- [Rewrite Every Pinned `gpt-5.4` Model ID Before 31 August 2026](model-selection/rewrite-pinned-gpt-5-4-model-ids-before-2026-08-31.md): GPT-5.4 leaves ChatGPT-authenticated Codex on 2026-08-31, and the failure surface is `~/.codex/agents/*.toml`, scheduled automations and managed defaults, which pin model IDs independently of the picker.
- [Try gpt-6.1-sol as the Codex default for repeated, long-running work before reaching for Astra](model-selection/try-gpt-6-1-sol-before-astra-for-long-codex-runs.md): Sol is the cheaper near-Astra model and became the bundled default model in Codex 0.159.1.
- [Type 'fast' in a Codex Chat to Toggle Fast Mode](model-selection/codex-fast-mode-type-fast-to-toggle.md): Typing the word "fast" as a message in a Codex thread flips the current session into fast mode; typing it again turns it back off.
- [Use Codex Ultrafast Only on Short Tasks, Because the Usage Cost Multiplier Grew With Task Complexity](model-selection/use-codex-ultrafast-only-for-short-tasks-its-usage-multiplier-grows-with-complexity.md): In one creator's tests, Codex's top speed tier was much faster but ate far more weekly usage on a long task. It was a better trade on a small one.
- [Walk a Skill Down the Model and Effort Ladder to Find the Cheapest One That Still Holds Quality](model-selection/walk-a-skill-down-the-model-and-effort-ladder-to-find-the-cost-floor.md): Test a skill starting on the strongest, most expensive model.
- [Plan with the larger model and spend the build tokens on the cheaper sibling, then use the big one to clean up](model-selection/plan-with-the-big-model-and-execute-with-the-cheaper-sibling.md): Mark Kashef's test (GPT-6.1 Sol vs Astra on a Codex build): similar time, $203 vs $43, so he recommends Astra plans and Sol executes.
- [In Codex, Change the Reasoning Level Mid-Thread: It No Longer Invalidates the Cache](model-selection/change-codex-reasoning-mid-thread-without-losing-the-cache.md): Codex's engineering lead says changing the reasoning level in Codex mid-conversation no longer invalidates the prompt cache, so raising effort for one hard step and lowering it after is now cheap.

### MCP

- [Expose Browser-Side WebMCP Tools From a Client-Side Web App Instead of Standing Up an MCP Server](mcp/expose-webmcp-tools-from-the-page-instead-of-an-mcp-server.md): Register agent tools in the page per the WebMCP spec and drive them as site tools from the desktop app's browser, instead of adding server infrastructure just to expose an MCP endpoint.
- [Migrate Off `codex mcp-server` Now: It Is Removed, and `app-server` Is Not Production-Supported](mcp/codex-mcp-server-is-removed-not-just-deprecated.md): removed 2026-09-05, and the replacement is marked experimental.
- [Reach a Private or Laptop-Local MCP Server With Secure MCP Tunnel Instead of Exposing a Public Endpoint](mcp/secure-mcp-tunnel-for-private-or-laptop-local-servers.md): A client next to your MCP server dials out over HTTPS, so the server never accepts inbound traffic, no public endpoint, no third-party tunnel vendor, no VPN.
- [Replace Per-Service MCP Servers With the Codex Chrome Extension](mcp/codex-chrome-extension-instead-of-service-mcp-servers.md): Rather than running Stripe, Vercel and Supabase MCP servers, let Codex drive those dashboards in your own browser through its Chrome extension, using the credentials you are already logged in with.

### Memory

- [After a Codex Failure, Ask It to Write Down What It Learned So It Doesn't Repeat It](memory/ask-codex-to-persist-failure-knowledge-to-memory.md): When Codex hits and works around an error (like a broken connection method), explicitly tell it to save that knowledge to a file in the project so future sessions don't rediscover it the hard way.

### Prompting

- [Draft the Goal Statement With the Agent Before You Start a Multi-Hour Goal Run](prompting/brainstorm-the-goal-statement-with-the-agent-before-firing-the-run.md): A vague goal like 'make this better' has no stopping condition and runs until you kill it, so sharpen the objective in conversation first, then fire it.
- [In Codex, Prefer Natural-Language Context Over @-Mentions](prompting/codex-prefer-natural-language-context-over-at-mentions.md): When prompting Codex and you don't already know the exact file, describe the goal in plain language rather than typing an @-mention, and let Codex semantically find the right context.
- [Write Codex Prompts as Goal, Context, Constraints, Done When](prompting/done-when-prompt-scaffold-for-codex.md): Codex's documented prompt shape has four parts, and the load-bearing one is the last: `done when` is the keyword that hands the agent its validation criteria, so it can check and heal its own work instead of declaring victory.

### Automation

- [After a Recurring Automation Runs, Ask It to Find Where It's Over-Orchestrated](automation/audit-recurring-automation-for-over-orchestration.md): Once an automation is working, ask the agent to review its own run and flag steps that don't need full agentic reasoning, like a routine weekly data refresh that could be a plain script.
- [Ask for Headless Browser Use When You Want to Keep Working, Headed When You Need to Watch](automation/ask-for-headless-browser-use-to-keep-working-in-the-foreground.md): Codex's browser agent runs either headed (visible in the app pane, you watch the mouse move) or headless (in the background), and you pick per task simply by asking for it in the prompt.
- [Check for a Native Codex Plugin Before Building a Custom API Integration](automation/prefer-codex-native-plugin-over-custom-api-integration.md): Before wiring up a service's raw API (creating cloud projects, API keys, OAuth), search Codex's plugin list; a native plugin is just a sign-in, while a missing plugin means doing the manual API setup yourself.
- [Default an Externally-Hosted Automation to a Deterministic Script, Reserve the Agent SDK for Genuinely Unbounded Tasks](automation/default-to-a-deterministic-script-reserve-the-agent-sdk-for-unbounded-loops.md): Most automations are a fixed sequence of steps with AI needed at only one or two of them, build those as a script that calls AI just at those points.
- [Have Computer Use Build You a CLI the First Time, Then Run the CLI on a Cheaper Model](automation/turn-a-computer-use-workflow-into-a-cli-then-run-it-on-a-cheaper-model.md): For a site with no API or MCP, let a strong model drive it once with computer use and reverse-engineer a reusable command line interface.
- [Host Recurring Codex Automations on an External Runner, Not as Codex Scheduled Tasks](automation/host-recurring-codex-automations-externally-not-as-scheduled-tasks.md): A Codex scheduled task sends its messages into a real chat thread, which consumes your weekly subscription usage.
- [Import Credentials Into Codex's Built-In Password Manager Instead of Pasting Them Into Chat](automation/import-a-password-csv-into-the-codex-browser-password-manager.md): Codex's in-app browser has a password manager you load from a CSV in settings, so a site that keeps logging you out (banks, anything with a short session) can still be automated without your password ever entering the chat transcript.
- [Keep a Codex Routine Local When the Model Matters, Cloud Tasks Cannot Set One](automation/codex-cloud-tasks-cannot-pin-model-or-project.md): Codex's cloud scheduled tasks keep running with the device offline but expose no chat, project, model or reasoning setting.
- [Pick the Codex Integration Layer by Job: codex exec for CI, the SDK for Code, App-Server for Products](automation/pick-the-codex-integration-layer-by-job-exec-sdk-app-server.md): codex exec for bounded non-interactive runs with structured output, the SDK to start/resume/stream from application code, app-server when the agent lifecycle is part of the product.
- [Point a Daily "Find Loops" Loop at Your Own Screen History to Discover What to Automate](automation/mine-screen-history-with-a-find-loops-loop.md): Enable Codex's Chronicle screen capture and run a scheduled loop that reads that history and recommends candidate loops back to you daily, you automate what you notice, and you don't notice your own routine.
- [Point a Family of Codex Scheduled Tasks at One Named Thread](automation/point-every-scheduled-task-at-one-codex-thread.md): When several routines are stages of one job, create a project, open a thread, rename it, and set every scheduled task to run inside that chat rather than each spawning its own.
- [Prefer the API, Except When It Is Metered or Mangles Formatting, Then Drive the Web UI](automation/drive-the-web-ui-when-the-api-is-metered-or-mangles-formatting.md): API-first is still the rule, but there are two named exceptions where browser use wins: the API charges per call while browser use rides your existing subscription, and the API handles rich formatting or media attachments badly.
- [Reach Mobile-Only Apps by Pointing Computer Use at the iPhone Mirroring Window](automation/drive-mobile-only-apps-through-iphone-mirroring.md): Open macOS iPhone Mirroring and tell Codex to drive it.
- [Stop and Ask an Automation What's Wrong Once It Runs Well Past Its Normal Time](automation/stop-and-diagnose-slow-unattended-automation.md): If a recurring automation that usually takes ~20 minutes is still running after 40+, interrupt it and ask what's happening rather than let it keep trying.
- [Supervise a Vision-Driven Browser Skill for About Ten Runs Before Putting It on a Schedule](automation/watch-a-vision-driven-browser-skill-for-ten-runs-before-scheduling-it.md): A browser skill captured from one successful natural-language run is not proven; watch it repeat roughly ten times and tighten its steps into strict instructions before you attach it to a scheduled routine.
- [Trigger Scheduled Tasks From Gmail, Slack, and GitHub Events Instead of Polling on a Timer](automation/trigger-scheduled-tasks-from-app-events-not-timers.md): Event triggers fire on the actual PR review or email instead of burning polled runs; an event-triggered task cannot also keep a time-based schedule, and near-simultaneous events may coalesce.
- [After Shipping a Feature in Codex, Add Usage Events and a Weekly Automation That Reports Adoption in Slack](automation/codex-ship-usage-events-then-schedule-a-weekly-adoption-report.md): In the shipping thread, ask Codex to add PostHog events for the feature, then after merge set an automation to message you weekly on Slack about usage.



### Commands

- [Learn Codex App's Five Keyboard Shortcuts: Threads, Palette, Terminal, Git Panel, Dictation](commands/codex-app-keyboard-shortcuts.md): Cmd+[ and Cmd+] move backward and forward between open threads in the Codex Electron app.
- [Press Cmd+J for an Inline Terminal Inside a Codex Thread](commands/codex-app-cmd-j-opens-an-inline-terminal.md): Cmd+J opens a terminal panel directly inside a Codex app thread, so you can run a command without leaving the thread or switching to a separate terminal app.
- [Press Cmd+K in the Codex App to Fuzzy-Find Any Setting or Command](commands/codex-app-cmd-k-opens-the-command-palette.md): Cmd+K opens a general find/command palette in the Codex app that jumps straight to Settings or runs any available command.
- [Press Cmd+Option+B to Open Codex App's Git Review Panel](commands/codex-app-git-review-panel-shortcut.md): Cmd+Option+B shows or hides the Codex app's git changes panel, where you stage, revert, and leave inline comments on a diff for the agent to iterate on.
- [You Can't Author a Codex Slash Command: Port Claude Code Commands as Skills](commands/codex-has-no-custom-slash-commands-port-them-as-skills.md): Codex's slash commands are built-in primitives only (model, plan mode, reasoning, review, personality, side).
- [Open the Codex Agent Command Center Without the Daemon via codex agents --no-daemon](commands/open-the-codex-command-center-without-the-daemon.md): `codex agents` is a session overview for browsing and switching sessions; `--no-daemon` runs it on an embedded app server, and unread results are now marked.

### Gotchas

- [Always Put API Keys in a Dot-Prefixed .env File, Never a Custom-Named Secrets File](gotchas/put-api-keys-only-in-dot-env-files.md): When Codex creates a credentials file, it's named .env.local (or .env); put your keys only there, not in an arbitrarily-named file like secrets.txt.
- [Codex Computer Use Is a Separate Plugin Install, and It Refuses OS Privacy Permissions](gotchas/codex-computer-use-installs-as-a-plugin-and-refuses-os-privacy-settings.md): Browser use is built in but computer use is not: install it from the plugins page first, expect a blue tint around your screen while it drives, and expect it to decline privacy-scoped OS settings and admin prompts.
- [Don't Assume a Lower Effort Level Spends Fewer Tokens](gotchas/effort-level-does-not-predict-token-spend.md): On the same prompt, Astra low burned about 15M tokens, medium about 10M and high about 14M.
- [Don't Enable Codex's "Locked Use" Capability on macOS: It Can Lock You Out of Your Keychain](gotchas/leave-codex-locked-use-off-on-macos.md): It rides on unstable macOS features and repeatedly locked swyx out of his keychain; leave it off until the underlying Apple bug is fixed.
- [Hard-Code a Repeated Automation's Destination Instead of Leaving It to the Agent's Judgment Each Run](gotchas/hardcode-the-destination-of-a-repeated-agent-action.md): An unattended agentic routine given open judgment over the same low-stakes decision every run (which channel to message, say) can drift over time: one creator's simple DM routine started sending to the wrong channels after about a month of unattended runs.
- [If Codex Ignores Your AGENTS.md, Check Directory Trust: Untrusted Projects No Longer Load It](gotchas/codex-untrusted-projects-no-longer-load-agents-md.md): As of CLI 0.150.0 an untrusted project's AGENTS.md silently never loads, so instruction-following failures in fresh checkouts are often a trust problem, not a prompt problem.
- [Keep API Keys in a Plain .env File Instead of Platform Plugin Connections, So They Move With the Automation](gotchas/env-file-keys-portable-across-hosting-targets.md): Codex's connected-account plugins do not transfer when you move an automation to a different host, so keys authorized that way have to be redone from scratch on the new platform.
- [Look at agent-made animations yourself, since it does not catch clipped edges](gotchas/review-agent-made-animations-by-eye-because-it-misses-clipping-edge-cases.md): Agents are poor at catching animation edge cases such as an element being cut off. Review frames manually.
- [Paste an API Key Into the .env File Codex Created, Never Into the Chat Message](gotchas/paste-secrets-into-the-env-file-itself-not-into-the-codex-chat.md): When Codex creates a placeholder key in .env, open that file (via the file browser's 'open in default app' or a text editor) and paste the real secret there directly, instead of typing or pasting the key into the chat prompt.
- [Replace codex exec --full-auto With --sandbox workspace-write, Which Was Removed Outright](gotchas/codex-full-auto-flag-removed-use-sandbox-workspace-write.md): CLI 0.147.0 deletes the deprecated flag rather than warning, so scripts, aliases and CI still passing it fail today.
- [Say 'Use Your Internal Browser' or Codex May Take Over Your Real Chrome Instead](gotchas/say-internal-browser-or-codex-may-grab-your-real-chrome.md): When you want Codex to browse in its own sandboxed browser, name it in the prompt.
- [Update the Codex app to get the destructive-command guardrails: the $HOME-deletion fixes ship client-side](gotchas/update-the-codex-app-to-get-the-destructive-command-guardrails.md): OpenAI's mitigations for Codex deleting user files (stricter deletion checks, harder-to-enable Full access, improved Auto-review) landed in client releases, so a pinned or stale Codex install still carries the old data-loss behavior.
- [Run codex doctor --network-only First When Codex Cannot Reach the Model](gotchas/run-codex-doctor-network-only-when-codex-cannot-reach-the-model.md): `codex doctor --network-only` checks config, network environment, provider HTTP and the Responses WebSocket without the full health report.

### Hooks

- [Add a Codex Interrupt Hook to Capture State When You Cut Off a Running Turn](hooks/add-a-codex-interrupt-hook-to-capture-state-on-esc.md): 0.150.0's Interrupt hook event runs a command or MCP handler when you Esc a top-level turn, so partial progress can be logged and orphaned processes cleaned up.
- [Codex App Hooks Fire on Five Lifecycle Events](hooks/codex-hook-lifecycle-events.md): Codex app hooks can target session start, pre-tool-use, post-tool-use, after a user submits a prompt, and when the agent stops.

## Cross-tool

### Workflows

- [A/B an Effort Level by Asking the Agent to Spin Up Renamed Parallel Threads Itself](workflows/spin-up-parallel-threads-at-different-effort-levels-from-one-prompt.md): Instead of opening several chats by hand, tell Codex (or Claude) to create N threads running the identical prompt, each at a different effort level, renamed in the sidebar and started at the same time, codex + claude-code.
- [Anchor an AI Language Port on a Runtime-Agnostic Test Suite Before Starting](workflows/anchor-an-ai-language-port-on-a-runtime-agnostic-test-suite.md): Bun's 64-agent Zig-to-Rust port was verifiable only because the TypeScript suite tests the runtime black-box; if your tests are written in the language being replaced, agents have no ground truth, claude-code + codex.
- [Budget a Manual Click-Through, Agent-Written Browser Tests Still Ship Obvious UI Bugs](workflows/click-through-the-app-yourself-agent-browser-tests-still-ship-ui-bugs.md): Both harnesses ran their own browser test suites (102 and 391 browser tests) and both still shipped navigation dead ends, dead controls and silent save failures that a few minutes of clicking found immediately, claude-code + codex.
- [Budget One Hero Clip for an Immersive Scroll Site, Then Let the Agent Scrub It Frame by Frame](workflows/one-hero-clip-scrubbed-by-scroll-for-an-immersive-site.md): The expensive input is a single clip (4.5-9 hours to render); everything after is deterministic code mapping frame index to scroll offset, so calibrate on a low-res test first, claude-code + codex.
- [Drag a Raw Video Into Claude Code or Codex and Tell It to Watch, Not Just Transcribe](workflows/feed-raw-video-to-claude-code-or-codex-and-say-watch-dont-just-transcribe.md): Both now ingest raw video natively (frames + audio), no Gemini hand-off; say "watch it end to end" or they only pull the transcript, claude-code + codex.
- [Explore Color and Type Pairings in Claude Design, Where You Can Click Through Them on the Live Design](workflows/explore-color-and-type-pairings-in-claude-design-not-in-code.md): Ask Claude Design to explore color and type pairings against your existing brand, then click between the pairings it produces and watch them apply to the real design, instead of editing color values in Claude Code, claude-design + claude-code.
- [Extract a Design System From Any Site With skill-ui at Zero Token Cost](workflows/extract-a-design-system-from-any-site-with-skill-ui.md): `npm install -g skill-ui` plus Playwright/Chromium, then `skill-ui --url <site> --mode ultra` statically extracts a full design system (skill.md, design.md, colors.json, spacing.json, fonts, component and animation references) with no API keys and no token spend, claude-design + claude-code.
- [Give Autonomous Agents a Safe Exit on Possibly-Impossible Tasks](workflows/give-autonomous-agents-a-safe-exit-on-impossible-tasks.md): 93% of the Hugging Face incident's rogue traffic traced to never-solved tasks; make "this appears unsolvable, stopping" a valid success condition and cap retries on stuck items, claude-code + codex.
- [Hand Off Claude Design Exports to Claude Code for Code-Level Iteration](workflows/hand-off-claude-design-exports-to-claude-code.md): When you need code-level changes, export the Claude Design project as a zip and continue in Claude Code. The technique stands; its old quota rationale does not (see below), claude-design + claude-code.
- [Make Video Proof Mandatory for UI-Changing PRs, Because Screenshot Self-Review Is Not QA](workflows/make-video-proof-mandatory-for-ui-changing-prs.md): Write it into AGENTS.md as a hard gate with a named escape hatch (state the exact blocker), captured from a real running surface rather than a test harness, claude-code + codex.
- [Pick /design in Claude Code When the Output Becomes Code, the Claude Design App When It Stays a Picture](workflows/choose-slash-design-or-the-claude-design-app-by-what-you-are-shipping.md): If the design becomes a real page or app in a codebase you already have open, use /design in Claude Code: the chosen artboard goes straight to implementation, claude-code + claude-design.
- [Point the Record-to-Skill Feature at Yourself and Tell It Not to Build a Skill: Just Audit](workflows/hijack-the-record-skill-to-audit-a-process-without-building-a-skill.md): Redirect screen-record-to-skill into an SOP-plus-automation audit of a manual process before deciding a skill is even the right primitive, claude-code + codex.
- [Point the Same Peer-Session Skill at Codex and Let Codex Coordinate Your Claude Code Sessions](workflows/coordinate-claude-code-sessions-from-codex-with-the-peer-session-skill.md): The mechanism runs over a socket, so the coordinator need not be Claude Code; swap pane vocabulary for thread vocabulary and ask from whichever tool you are already in, claude-code + codex.
- [Read Local Agent Logs With ccusage to See Spend Across Every CLI You Run](workflows/audit-cross-tool-token-spend-with-ccusage.md): ccusage reads the local logs of Claude Code, Codex, Gemini, Goose and OpenCode and reports models, input and output tokens, totals and cost per time period in one place, claude-code + codex.
- [Read the tool's entire docs and trial every feature in deliberate exploration phases, don't rely on real work to surface them](workflows/read-the-full-docs-in-deliberate-exploration-phases.md): Real work only surfaces the features you stumble into; periodically read the full docs end to end and try every hotkey, and re-run the pass when a paradigm-shifting release lands, claude-code + codex.
- [Run 3-5 Parallel Sessions Per Project, and Expect About Four to Be Your Own Supervision Ceiling](workflows/how-many-parallel-claude-sessions-to-actually-run.md): Instead of running one Claude Code session at a time, keep 3-5 instances per project (10-12 total across projects) running in parallel so you're never idle waiting on one agent, claude-code + codex.
- [Run a Dedicated Clarify Pass on a Spec Before Planning](workflows/spec-kits-clarify-phase-catches-spec-ambiguity-early.md): GitHub Spec Kit inserts a distinct 'clarify' step between writing the spec and planning the build, specifically to surface anything ambiguous that would otherwise let the model make an unstated decision on its own, claude-code + codex.
- [Run Codex Browser QA Against the Running App While Claude Keeps Building - It's Read-Only, So They Don't Collide](workflows/run-codex-browser-qa-concurrently-while-claude-keeps-building.md): Point Codex's computer use at the localhost app with 'click every button, try to break it, find bugs' while Claude Code keeps writing features in the same folder; because the QA pass only reads and clicks, the two sessions coexist as long as each is told what the other owns, claude-code + codex.
- [Scaffold Your Target Framework Before Pasting the Design Handoff](workflows/scaffold-the-target-framework-before-pasting-the-design-handoff.md): Claude Design emits plain HTML and CSS, so scaffold your real stack first and name it in the handoff prompt. The export then gets ported into your framework rather than dumped in as foreign markup, claude-design + claude-code.
- [Treat Any Public Hint of a Security Bug as a Live Exploit: Agents Weaponize It in Minutes](workflows/treat-public-hints-of-a-security-bug-as-live-exploits.md): Agent watchers turned a public patch discussion into live probes within ~10 minutes; prepare security fixes in a private fork or advisory and compress disclosure-to-release to zero, claude-code + codex.
- [Use a Cross-Provider Orchestrator to Run Claude Code and Codex Side by Side](workflows/cross-provider-agent-orchestrator-across-worktrees-with-existing-subscriptions.md): Orchestrators like Orca run each task in its own git worktree across Claude Code, Codex, and others from one UI on your existing subscriptions, with an orchestration skill for cross-provider handoff; carries a ToS caution for subscription auth, claude-code + codex.
- [Use Open Design as a Local-First, Agent-Agnostic Alternative to Claude Design](workflows/open-design-local-first-agent-agnostic-claude-design-alternative.md): Open Design is a free open-source Claude Design clone that runs locally on your own coding agent (Claude Code/Codex/Cursor/Gemini) and API key, ships 71 built-in design systems plus custom-skill support, can auto-generate images/video via pluggable media APIs, and outputs raw HTML files you drop straight into your project, claude-design + claude-code.
- [Verify Every New User Flow After a PR With an Isolated Sandbox, a Cheap Browser Model, and a Judge Agent](workflows/per-user-flow-sandbox-pipeline-with-a-cheap-browser-model-and-judge-agent.md): Enumerate the flows a PR changed, give each its own throwaway sandbox, and split seed/drive/judge across three agents so the browser step can run on a cheap model at ~1/17th the cost, claude-code + codex.
- [Connect Claude and Codex by Installing Both CLIs and Telling Each to Call the Other](workflows/connect-claude-and-codex-by-installing-both-clis-no-plugin.md): Mark says that with both CLIs installed, each desktop app can call the other's headless CLI: in Claude a prompt names 'the Codex CLI', in Codex it names the Claude CLI, no plugin or skill.
- [Audit Your Past Sessions With a Keyword Script Over the JSONL Files, Not Subagents Reading Every Conversation](workflows/script-scan-session-jsonl-for-pushback-instead-of-fanning-out-subagents.md): To learn where you pushed back on or got frustrated with the agent, have it write a script that scans the session JSONL files for pushback keywords and for which tools were or were not called, claude-code + codex.
- [Give a Multi-Hour Goal a Set of Design Screens as Its Exit Criteria](workflows/use-design-screens-as-the-exit-criteria-for-a-long-goal.md): For a UI build that runs overnight, generate the full set of screens first and let the agent cross-reference its running app against them, instead of a vague 'build the app' finish line, claude-code + codex.
- [Run Contract-Test Sensors During Development for a Cheaper Model, but Only Once as a Final Gate for a Frontier Model](workflows/run-contract-test-sensors-continuously-for-a-cheap-model-and-once-for-a-frontier-model.md): In a 10-run Codex experiment, machine-readable API contracts plus contract tests took a cost-optimized model from 3 of 10 to 10 of 10 accepted runs, while for a frontier model the same harness added cost with no gain, claude-code + codex.
- [When an Agent Applies a Temporary Workaround, Have It Write a Dated Back-Out Reminder Into AGENTS.md](workflows/have-the-agent-write-a-dated-back-out-reminder-into-agents-md.md): After an agent applies a stopgap, have it add a dated, self-contained section to AGENTS.md saying what to check on or after a date, how to remove the workaround, how to verify, and to delete the section afterwards, claude-code + codex.
- [Treat a Red Build as a Stop Signal When Agents Run in Parallel, and Fix the Cause Instead of Living With Red](workflows/treat-a-red-build-as-a-stop-signal-when-agents-run-in-parallel.md): In an experiment where agents wrote all the code, the app ran but the build was frequently red, which made a green build stop being reassuring too, claude-code + codex.

### Agents

- [Let Only Your Strongest-Judgment Model Talk to Humans, Enforced at the Slack/Email Boundary](agents/let-only-the-strongest-model-talk-to-humans.md): In a mixed-model fleet, what fails in human contact is judgment, not coding; give only the top-tier model the channel credentials, enforced at the integration layer, claude-code + codex.

### Skills

- [After Correcting a Skill, Diagnose the Cause and Update the Smallest Durable Place, Not Just the Output](skills/diagnose-and-update-the-smallest-durable-place-after-a-skill-correction.md): When you correct a skill's result, don't just say fix it, claude-code + codex.
- [Battle-Test an Important Skill in a Second, Compatible Agent Harness](skills/battle-test-a-skill-in-a-second-agent-harness.md): Run a heavily-used skill through a different compatible harness (Claude Code and Codex, for example) on the same task and compare, claude-code + codex.
- [Bracket Claude Design With Claude Code Skills: It Cannot Load Your Own](skills/bracket-claude-design-with-claude-code-skills.md): Claude Design is a separate app that supports no custom skills; it always runs Anthropic's built-in front-end design skill, claude-design + claude-code.
- [Put Every Skill One Level Under `skills/` - Nested Skill Directories Are Never Discovered](skills/put-every-skill-one-level-under-skills.md): Discovery is strictly one level deep, so grouping skills into category subfolders silently loses all of them with no error, claude-code + codex.
- [Skip the Frontend Design Skill for Whole Sites, Build the Design System in Claude Design First](skills/skip-the-frontend-design-skill-for-whole-sites.md): Do not reach for the built-in frontend design skill to generate a website, claude-code + claude-design.
- [For a One-Off Task, Name the Repo in the Prompt Instead of Installing It as a Skill](skills/point-the-agent-at-a-repo-for-one-task-instead-of-installing-it-as-a-skill.md): When a trending repo or skill might help with one job, tell the agent to refer to it for that task rather than adding it to your permanent skill set, claude-code + codex.

### Plugins

- [Repackage a Plugin as Root `plugin.json` + `skills/` + `mcp.json` to Ship It Everywhere](plugins/repackage-plugins-to-the-agent-plugins-spec.md): Codex 0.147.0 installs portable Agent Plugins, so the same package loads in Codex, VS Code and Cursor; only skills and MCP servers travel, everything else goes under a reverse-domain `extensions` namespace, claude-code + codex.

### Configuration

- [Claude Code Reads AGENTS.md When a Directory Has No CLAUDE.md, but Import It Explicitly If You Disable Telemetry](configuration/claude-code-falls-back-to-agents-md-import-it-to-be-sure.md): Since 2.1.277 Claude Code falls back to AGENTS.md in a directory with no CLAUDE.md, so a Codex-first repo no longer needs a symlink; but the fallback sat behind a remote flag that defaulted off until 2.1.281, so with telemetry or nonessential traffic disabled it was silently skipped, claude-code + codex.
- [Escalate an Agent Rule One Tier Each Re-Violation: Custom, Warning, Written Law, Then Mechanical Enforcement](configuration/escalate-agent-rules-one-enforcement-tier-per-violation.md): Yegge's 50-agent fleet promotes a rule one enforcement tier per re-violation, keeping the mechanically-enforced surface small while repeat offenders eventually hit a fence, claude-code + codex.
- [Migrate a Claude Code Setup Into Codex With `/import`, Then Turn On Settings > Import Sync](configuration/import-claude-code-into-codex-and-turn-on-sync.md): `/import` is a standing command, not a one-time offer, and the automatic-updates toggle is what stops the imported skills and MCP servers drifting, claude-code + codex.
- [Port a Project Across Claude Code and Codex in Three Layers: Only One of Them Actually Changes](configuration/port-a-project-between-claude-code-and-codex-by-layer.md): Split your project into shared knowledge (docs, references, scripts, every agent reads these unchanged), skills and agents (same content, different location), and tool-specific config (genuinely different files), claude-code + codex.
- [Raise a Local Runtime's Context Length to 32K–64K Before Agentic Coding: the Default Is Far Too Small](configuration/raise-local-runtime-context-length-to-at-least-32k.md): Runtimes ship a chat-sized context that silently caps agent runs, and the KV cache buys that headroom out of the same RAM as the weights, claude-code + codex.
- [Replace a Read-These-Docs-Before-Every-Edit Rule With Per-Topic Pointers](configuration/point-agents-md-at-docs-contextually-not-before-every-edit.md): a blanket read rule burns context on every trivial change, codex + claude-code.
- [Skip Theme Factory Presets, Generate a Real Design System in Claude Design and Export It](configuration/skip-theme-factory-presets-build-the-design-system-in-claude-design.md): Anthropic's theme factory presets carry no design tokens and no heading scale and are shared by everyone using them, so build the system in Claude Design's design systems feature instead and drop the export into your context folder, claude-design + claude-code.
- [Source a Palette From Huemint and a Font Pairing From Font Pair to Escape the AI-Default Inter Look](configuration/source-palette-from-huemint-and-fonts-from-font-pair.md): When you have no existing brand, pull a colour palette from Huemint and a font pairing from Font Pair instead of letting the tool choose, specifically to avoid the default Inter that AI design tools reach for, claude-design + claude-code.
- [Turn Tacit Know-How Into Playbook Files Referenced From AGENTS.md Instead of Skills](configuration/dictate-process-into-playbook-files-and-point-agents-md-at-them.md): Mark Kashef had a client dictate her launch and webinar process for an hour, saved it as two plain markdown files in a playbooks folder, and had AGENTS.md reference them for relevant tasks, rather than building skills, claude-code + codex.
- [Test CLAUDE.md With Five Probe Tasks in Fresh Sessions Instead of Rewriting It Again](configuration/test-claude-md-with-five-probe-tasks-in-fresh-sessions.md): Instead of growing the instruction file after each miss, keep a fixed set of five probe prompts, run each in a fresh session, kill it as soon as you see the first moves, and change one line only when a probe fails, claude-code + codex.

### Context Management

- [On a Local Model, a Bloated System Prompt Is a Latency Tax: Prune It and Compress the KV Cache](context-management/prune-the-system-prompt-when-an-agent-runs-on-a-local-model.md): Every system-prompt token is re-processed by your own hardware each turn; pruning an agent's oversized prompt took a 14B model's reply to "hi" from ~2 minutes to ~30 seconds, claude-code + codex.
- [Pre-compute the Design System Outside Claude Design, Then Import It Finished](context-management/precompute-the-design-system-outside-claude-design.md): Claude Design's weekly quota is a separate, small pool, and design-system setup burns more of it than anything else you'll do, claude-design + claude-code.
- [When One Tool Is Stuck, Hand the Session to the Other One: Not Just to a Fresh Context](context-management/hand-a-stuck-session-to-the-other-tool-with-a-handoff-file.md): Keep a session-handoff skill that dumps what you discussed, the active files, the decisions made, and the next steps into a file you can paste straight into the other tool, claude-code + codex.
- [End Sessions With a /handoff That Marks One File as Latest, and Start the Next Tool With /prime](context-management/write-one-latest-handoff-file-and-prime-the-other-tool-from-it.md): Mark replaced accumulating plan.md files with a /handoff skill that records where you are, what was decided and the next step, plus a note that it is the latest.

### Model Selection

- [Check the Sample Size Before You Re-Route Work to a Hyped New Model](model-selection/check-the-sample-size-before-rerouting-to-a-hyped-model.md): Ox Alpha's viral 80% score came from a community run over roughly eight questions, claude-code + codex.
- [Have a Frontier Agent Build and Keep Improving the Harness Your Local Model Runs In](model-selection/build-and-harden-a-local-harness-with-a-frontier-agent.md): The local model is not the bottleneck, the harness is; have Codex or Claude Code author the task management, self-check loop and skills, then let the cheap model execute on them, claude-code + codex.
- [If Codex Only Audits, the $20 ChatGPT Tier Is Enough: Keep the Max Spend on Your Daily Driver](model-selection/pair-a-max-claude-plan-with-the-cheap-chatgpt-tier-for-audit-only-codex.md): Reviewing a plan or a diff costs a fraction of what generating code costs, so a Codex-as-auditor setup runs fine on the $20 ChatGPT plan (reviews even work on the free tier) alongside a $100 Claude Max plan, claude-code + codex.
- [Keep Closed-Source Models for Planning and Review, Route Mechanical Edits to a Cheap Open-Weight Workhorse](model-selection/route-mechanical-tasks-to-cheap-open-weight-workhorse.md): Use Claude/Codex for detailed planning, bug review, and testing, but deliberately offload small mechanical tasks (renaming a batch of files, grep-style searches) to a cheap open-weight model like GLM accessed via OpenRouter, to cut cost and token spend without touching quality-sensitive work, claude-code + codex.
- [Route Architecture Decisions on Big New Projects to GPT via Codex](model-selection/route-architecture-planning-to-gpt-for-big-new-projects.md): For a project expected to grow large, route the up-front architecture and structuring decisions to GPT-5.2 via Codex even when Opus is doing the actual implementation, claude-code + codex.
- [Route Cross-Browser, Property, and Fault-Injection Test Coverage to Codex: Claude Code's Default Suite Skips It](model-selection/codex-adds-cross-browser-and-fault-injection-tests-by-default.md): In a head-to-head build, Codex added cross-browser testing, property tests, and fault injections unprompted, while Claude Code's default test suite covered security and data-correctness but not mobile/cross-browser or fault injection, claude-code + codex.
