- [Structure Agent Teams So Teammates Never Touch the Same Files](agents/agent-teams-agent-should-not-touch-same-files.md): File conflicts between agents are the primary failure mode of agent teams; design team roles around domain isolation, not component ownership.
- [Tell an Orchestrator Model to Never Write or Execute Code Itself So It Can Run All Day](agents/pure-delegator-orchestrator-preserves-context-across-a-long-session.md): A coordinator that also edits files burns context fast; forbidding it from writing or running anything keeps a multi-hour session well under the context ceiling.
- [The /agents Wizard Is Gone: Edit .claude/agents/ or Ask Claude to Manage Subagents](agents/agents-wizard-removed-edit-agents-dir.md): Claude Code removed the interactive `/agents` wizard (2.1.198); scaffold subagents by writing `.claude/agents/<name>.md` or asking Claude to do it.
- [The Built-In 'general-purpose' Subagent Runs on Sonnet With Full Read/Write Tools](agents/general-purpose-subagent-runs-full-tool-sonnet.md): Besides Explore (read-only, and since 2.1.198 on your main model rather than Haiku) and the plan-mode research agent, Claude Code has a third automatic built-in subagent called general-purpose that runs on Sonnet with full tool access, and Claude delegates to it on its own for complex multi-step tasks that need both exploring the codebase and editing across multiple files.
- [Use /rewind to Undo a Forked Subagent's Result You Didn't Want](agents/rewind-to-undo-an-unhelpful-fork.md): If a forked subagent's answer isn't helpful, run /rewind to go back to before you spun it up, since a fork's spawn and result are part of the main conversation's timeline.
- [Use a Fork Instead of /btw When a Side Question Needs Tool Calls](agents/fork-vs-btw-for-multistep-tool-calling-asides.md): /btw (or /bytheway) only supports a single-turn answer with no tool calls, while a forked subagent can run multiple steps and call tools like MCP servers; pick a fork whenever the aside needs research or tool use.
- [Use a Fresh-Context Subagent for Design Work to Avoid Code Bias](agents/design-subagent-fresh-context-to-avoid-code-bias.md): A subagent that hasn't seen your existing code generates more distinct designs; the main session's code context biases toward what already exists.
- [Use a Separate Evaluator Agent That Exercises the Running App, Not Self-Review](agents/separate-evaluator-agent-exercises-the-running-app.md): Split generation and verification across two agents; give the evaluator browser automation to click through the real app and concrete runtime thresholds, instead of letting the author grade its own work.
- [Use Agent Teams Only for 7-8/10 Complexity Tasks Needing Cross-Agent Communication](agents/agent-teams-for-cross-domain-collaboration.md): Teams enable agent-to-agent messaging; they cost 4-7x more tokens than a single session, reserve for genuinely collaborative multi-domain work.
- [Use Nested Subagents (2-3 Layers) for Multi-Order Effect Analysis](agents/nested-subagents-for-layered-analysis.md): For cascading questions (change → implications → blast radius), let layer-1 subagents spawn layer-2 subagents so each analysis level stays in its own context.
- [Use the Built-In @bash Specialized Subagent for Batch or Multi-Step Bash Work](agents/bash-specialized-subagent-for-batch-commands.md): Typing @bash spawns a subagent purpose-built for running batch/multi-step shell commands (e.g. npm run build), returning only the final result to the main session and keeping its context clean; the same subagent can be assigned via agent: bash in a skill's frontmatter.
- [Wrap Your Terminal's CLI in a Skill So Claude Code Launches and Steers Its Own Peer Sessions](agents/wrap-your-terminal-cli-in-a-skill-so-claude-code-spawns-peer-sessions.md): Claude Code can message other sessions but cannot open one; teach it your multiplexer's CLI in a skill and work fans out into named panes you can watch, message and follow up on.
- [Write `--help` output that tells a coding agent everything it needs](agents/design-cli-help-for-agents.md): When building CLI tools Claude Code will drive, make --help self-contained so the agent learns the whole tool from running it. (Simon Willison).
- [You Must Explicitly Ask for an Agent Team, Claude Won't Auto-Select One](agents/agent-teams-require-explicit-request-not-auto-selected.md): Unlike Explore/Plan/general-purpose subagents which Claude delegates to automatically based on task complexity, Agent Teams is never chosen on its own; you must say in your prompt that you want an agent team.
- [Ask for a Subagent's Effort Level in the Delegation: the Agent Tool Takes an effort Parameter](agents/set-a-subagent-effort-level-through-the-agent-tool.md): Since 2.1.292 the Agent tool has an `effort` parameter, so Claude runs a subagent at the effort level you ask for instead of the session's.
- [Give a Context-Heavy Subagent Its Own autoCompactWindow in Frontmatter](agents/give-a-context-heavy-subagent-its-own-autocompactwindow.md): Since 2.1.296 subagent frontmatter and `--agents` definitions accept `autoCompactWindow`, so a subagent can auto-compact earlier than the main conversation.
- [After Writing an Agent File, Check claude --debug for Misspelled Frontmatter Fields](agents/check-claude-debug-for-misspelled-agent-frontmatter-fields.md): Since 2.1.296 `--debug` names unrecognized frontmatter fields in custom agent files, with a hint for likely typos.
- [Run a Persona Council as Subagents Talking to a Chair, Not as an Agent Team](agents/persona-council-with-subagents-and-a-chair-not-agent-teams.md): Nate Herk runs his seven-advisor decision council as subagents with the main Claude Code session as chair, after finding agent teams gave very similar answers at higher cost.
- [Keep a Persona Subagent File Short and Route It to a Per-Persona Knowledge Folder](agents/thin-persona-subagent-file-routing-to-a-knowledge-folder.md): Each advisor in Nate Herk's council has a short subagent file that says how the person thinks, the one question they own and how they decide, then links to a folder of markdown files holding their rules, frameworks, positions, stories, voice and sources.
- [Delete Custom Agents That Are Only a Role Title; Keep the Ones That Carry Knowledge or a Tool Restriction](agents/delete-role-only-custom-agents-keep-ones-with-knowledge-or-tool-limits.md): An agent defined as 'you are a senior copywriter' adds nothing a current model lacks. Keep agents that encode a checklist of your process or a restricted tool set, and let one orchestrating agent decide when to split work.
- [When Claude Builds Interactive Teaching Demos, Chain a Builder, a Clarity Reviewer and a Domain-Accuracy Reviewer](agents/add-a-domain-accuracy-reviewer-subagent-after-a-builder-for-generated-interactive-demos.md): When some of Claude's interactive diagrams and demos came out wrong and confusing, Ray had each chapter go through a builder, then a clarity reviewer, then a subject-accuracy reviewer (physics, in his case).
- [Never Let a Big-Context Subagent Wait on a Slow Command: Run the Wait From the Main Session](agents/run-slow-waits-from-the-main-session-not-a-big-context-subagent.md): Subagent requests use a 5-minute prompt cache even on a subscription, so a subagent that waits on a long test run re-writes its whole context on its next turn; run slow commands from the main session and hand failures to a fresh agent.
- [Hand a Code Reviewer Only the Spec and Diff, and a Verifier Only the Flow to Check](agents/give-reviewers-only-the-spec-and-diff-and-verifiers-only-the-flow.md): A review agent in a fresh window should see the original spec and the diff, not the implementer's reasoning.
- [Use a Coordinated Agent Swarm for Independent Search Work Like Bug and Vulnerability Hunting, Not for Building](agents/use-agent-swarms-only-for-independent-search-tasks.md): Ray cites an Anthropic result where a coordinated swarm found 266 vulnerabilities for 27 million tokens versus 21 for 6.5 million with simple parallel fan-out.

### Skills

- [A Skill's agent: Frontmatter Field Launches It Inside a Named Subagent, Not Just a Fork](skills/skill-agent-field-runs-the-skill-inside-a-named-subagent.md): Setting agent: in a skill's frontmatter (with context fork enabled) spins up that specific subagent, complete with its own system prompt, and then triggers the skill running inside that subagent's context.
- [Add a Config-File Setup Wizard to Skills You Share With Others](skills/add-a-setup-wizard-to-shared-skills.md): A shared skill can ask the user for its own per-user configuration (like a channel handle) the first time it's used, resolve it via an API, then write it to a config.json it reads on every future run.
- [Add a contents list at the top of any skill reference file over 100 lines](skills/add-a-contents-list-to-skill-reference-files-over-100-lines.md): Claude may preview a long reference file instead of reading it all, so rules past the preview can be missed. A contents list at the top shows the full scope even on a partial read.
- [Add a Log File to Skills So They Remember Previous Runs](skills/skill-log-file-for-continuity-between-runs.md): A skills/[name]/data/log.json or learnings.md file lets the skill track what it wrote last time, avoiding duplicate content and maintaining continuity.
- [Add a Pass/Fail Verdict Step to the End of Custom Skills, Not Just Actions](skills/pass-fail-verdict-step-in-custom-skills.md): When writing your own skill (not the built-in /verify), end it with an explicit verdict step that states pass or fail, instead of stopping once the described actions are done.
- [Add Pre-Built API Scripts to Skills to Save Tokens on Repeated Data Fetching](skills/skills-add-scripts-to-avoid-repeated-api-scaffolding.md): Build Python/Node scripts once for each API your skill uses; subsequent runs call the scripts rather than re-deriving the API pattern from scratch.
- [Apply the Inner-Loop Test Before Turning a Task Into a Skill](skills/skill-frequency-test-inner-loop.md): Build a skill only if you do the task 2-3+×/week, it follows the same pattern each time, and preloaded context would improve output; otherwise re-prompt and avoid skill sprawl.
- [Ask the 'Wrong Primitive' Question - a Skill Is a Suggestion, a Hook/Function Is a Rule](skills/ask-the-wrong-primitive-question-before-building-a-skill.md): Before packaging something as a skill, decide whether it should instead be a rule, a CLAUDE.md line, a CLI command, a slash command, or a hook - gate on determinism: predictable same-every-time steps belong in a hook or Python function (a deterministic rule), not a skill (a probabilistic suggestion the model may or may not follow).
- [Audit Your Skill and MCP Loadout With drskill Before It Silently Degrades Routing](skills/audit-your-skill-and-mcp-loadout-with-drskill.md): Loadouts accrete (one enterprise agent auto-loaded 600+ inherited skills); scan per-harness, LLM-judge overlap with `--deep`, and audit traces for what never fires.
- [Before Installing a Downloaded Skill Pack, Have Claude Recommend a Subset Biased Toward Fewer](skills/curate-a-bulk-skill-pack-before-installing-not-after.md): Bulk-installing 100 skills creates testing debt and loads every description into context; curate before install, then re-audit periodically for overlap.
- [Before Writing a Skill, Ask Claude Whether an Established Term or Framework Already Names What You Want](skills/ask-claude-for-the-established-term-before-writing-a-skill-from-scratch.md): A word the model already knows deeply replaces paragraphs of explanation. A made-up word recruits no priors.
- [Budget Skill Descriptions Against a Shared Listing Budget and a Per-Skill Cap](skills/skill-descriptions-share-a-15000-character-ceiling.md): Skill descriptions share one listing budget, and each description has its own cap. On Claude Code 2.1.281 the budget is 1% of the context window (in characters) and each description is capped at 1,536 characters; longer ones are cut off. Keep descriptions short, trigger words first, and prune marginal skills.
- [Build 'Clone-the-Reviewer' Skills From a Real Person's Own Writing to Pre-Review Your Work](skills/clone-a-real-stakeholder-skill-for-pre-submission-feedback.md): For non-code deliverables, build a skill that clones the specific real person who will judge the work, your actual manager (from her blog posts, Slack messages, emails), the end user/customer, or named vertical experts, from their real artifacts, so you get their likely feedback and can revise before you ever submit.
- [Build a Self-Interview ('Grill Me') Skill to Extract Tacit Knowledge Into Context](skills/self-interview-skill-to-extract-tacit-knowledge.md): A skill that interviews you with 20-30 pointed questions until it stops learning, then writes the answers to context/skill files, turns in-your-head process into reusable context.
- [Cap a router skill at about five branches, then ask Claude whether to split it](skills/cap-router-skills-at-five-branches.md): Past ~5 branches a context-routing skill is over-engineered; ask whether to simplify or break it up.
- [Classify Each Skill as Utility, Verification, Data-Enrichment, or Orchestration](skills/classify-skills-into-four-types.md): Keep each skill to one of the four types; a skill that both does and grades its own work confuses Claude about when to invoke it and what "done" means.
- [Compose Small Skills Into a Skill System, Not One Monolith](skills/skill-systems-over-monolithic-skills.md): Chain small single-purpose skills through an orchestrator skill (defining order, per-step inputs, and output→input handoffs) instead of baking everything into one monolithic process skill.
- [Convert a Screen Recording Into an SOP Before Handing It to skill-creator](skills/turn-a-recording-into-an-sop-first-then-hand-that-to-skill-creator.md): Do not ask the agent to turn a recording straight into a skill.
- [Create a KB-Only Skill That Refuses to Answer From General Training Knowledge](skills/scope-a-skill-to-answer-only-from-your-knowledge-base.md): Claude blends ingested docs with training data and averages your specific answer down to generic best practice; scope a skill to one directory and forbid outside knowledge.
- [Create a Skill Only for Something You Keep Sending Back, and Mine Your History to Find It](skills/create-a-skill-only-for-a-correction-you-keep-having-to-make.md): Matt Pocock's definition: a skill is a correction you got tired of making. If the model already gets the task right first time, the skill is just token cost.
- [Delete a Skill If You Can't State When Claude Should Pick It Over a Similar One](skills/explainability-test-to-prune-overlapping-skills.md): When auditing an installed skill library, apply a one-sentence test to every pair of similar skills: if you cannot explain when Claude should use one versus the other, delete one of them.
- [Don't Ask a Model to Rebuild Someone's System From a Video Transcript](skills/dont-build-a-skill-from-a-video-transcript.md): Pasting a YouTuber's transcript into a model and asking it to build you their skill or workflow reliably blows up: a transcript carries no artifacts, no acceptance criteria, and no integration checks.
- [Don't Author a claude.ai Skill That Relies on `!` Bash or `@` File Expansion](skills/claude-ai-synced-skills-lose-bash-and-file-expansion.md): Synced skills run locally with those directives inert, so the skill loads and looks right while the pre-computed context it depended on is simply absent.
- [Don't Try to Run Skills in Parallel: They Share the Main Context Window](skills/dont-parallelize-skills-they-share-context.md): Because skills execute as tools inside the same session, running several in parallel quickly fills up that one context window; use subagents instead when you need true parallel work.
- [Find No-Op Lines in a Skill by Deleting One and Checking Whether the Output Changes](skills/find-no-op-lines-in-a-skill-by-deleting-one-and-rerunning.md): Lines such as 'be thorough' are no-ops: if removing one changes nothing, it was never doing anything and costs tokens.
- [Fix one noun per entity in a skill before writing it, inconsistent naming causes strange behavior](skills/fix-one-noun-per-entity-in-a-skill-before-writing-it.md): Name each entity in a skill once (map, ticket, session) and use that exact word everywhere in the file, because reusing one word for several things or one thing under several words degrades the agent's behavior.
- [Fork /init Into One Scaffolding Skill Per Project Archetype You Actually Start](skills/fork-init-into-project-archetype-scaffolding-skills.md): Generic /init produces a generic CLAUDE.md and no scaffolding; build a named variant per project type you start often so you stop re-deriving last project's decisions.
- [Generate Narrowly-Scoped Library Skills From Current Docs](skills/context7-skill-wizard-scoped-library-skills.md): Use Context7's skill wizard to turn up-to-date docs into a skill scoped to ONE aspect (e.g. Clerk sign-in flow); you can't hand-author a skill for a library you don't deeply know.
- [Give a design skill an image/video-model API key so it autogenerates the assets the brief lacks](skills/wire-an-image-video-api-key-into-a-design-skill-for-missing-assets.md): An aggregator key (kie.ai) in the project env lets the skill generate missing images, convert them to video, and stitch clips itself.
- [Give a Shared Sub-Skill a Mode Argument Instead of Cloning It Per Caller](skills/give-a-reusable-sub-skill-a-mode-argument.md): When a skill will be reused by several orchestrators, pass the purpose in as an argument rather than hard-coding it.
- [Give a skill a check, fix, repeat loop, and use a style guide as the checker when there is no code](skills/give-a-skill-a-check-fix-repeat-loop-with-a-style-guide-as-the-checker.md): Have the skill draft, review against a reference document, note each issue with the section it breaks, revise, and only finalize when everything passes.
- [Give an Editing Skill a Scoring Rubric and a Revise Threshold Instead of Dozens of Examples](skills/give-an-editing-skill-a-rubric-and-a-revise-threshold.md): The Stop Slop skill is core rules plus a few quick checks, then a 1-to-10 score across five named dimensions (rhythm, trust, authenticity, density and one more) for a total out of 50, with anything under 35 sent back for revision against the dimension that scored worst.
- [Give an Evaluation Skill a Blunt Mode and a Professional Mode You Select at Invocation](skills/give-a-critique-skill-a-blunt-mode-and-a-professional-mode.md): When you encode a critique framework as a skill, expose the delivery tone as a mode argument, so you can ask for the harsh read when you want the truth and the measured read when you need to act on it.
- [Give an order-dependent skill a copyable checklist with a go-back step](skills/give-an-ordered-skill-a-checklist-with-a-go-back-step.md): For multistep skills where order matters, have Claude copy a short checklist into its reply and tick it off. Add a line that sends it back to an earlier step when a check fails.
- [Give each child planning session exactly two documents: a map and a ticket](skills/give-each-child-planning-session-exactly-two-documents-a-map.md): When writing an orchestrator skill, define the child's context as precisely two artifacts, a map of decisions already made, plus one ticket carrying its specific task, rather than handing down a single blob of context.
- [Grow a Skill by One-Sentence Fixes for Failures You Watched, and Add Nothing You Have Not Seen Fail](skills/grow-a-skill-with-one-sentence-fixes-for-failures-you-actually-watched.md): Pocock does not run evals. They use the skill on real work for about a week and add one line per observed break.
- [Harden a Plan Document by Running Specialist Skills Against It](skills/layer-specialist-skills-onto-plan-document.md): Point domain skills at plan.md (not just code) to revise the plan before implementation, an API skill drafts a migration plan, a Postgres skill rewrites it to DB best practices.
- [Have Claude Code Audit Your Skill Descriptions for Overlap, Then Verify With Three Prompts](skills/audit-skill-descriptions-with-claude-then-three-prompt-test.md): Ask Claude Code to review every installed skill's description for what it does, when it should trigger, and where it overlaps with other skills, rewriting only the ambiguous ones.
- [Install Marketplace Skills With `npx skills add <dir> --skill <name>`, and a Local Flag to Scope It](skills/npx-skills-add-cli-installer-local-or-global.md): The community `skills` CLI installs a specific skill from a marketplace directory with `npx skills add <marketplace>/<dir> --skill <skill-name>`; adding a local-scope flag (heard in the video as `--local`/`-lo`) installs it into the current project's `.claude/skills` instead of your global skills folder.
- [Keep skill reference files one level deep, linked straight from SKILL.md](skills/link-every-skill-reference-file-directly-from-skill-md.md): If SKILL.md points to a file that points to a second file, Claude may only preview the second one. Link every reference file directly from SKILL.md.
- [Keep SKILL.md and Reference Files Separate So You Can Localize Quality Issues](skills/separate-skill-md-from-references-to-localize-debugging.md): Splitting the process (SKILL.md) from the knowledge (reference files) lets you tell whether a bad output is a process bug or a knowledge gap; a single monolithic SKILL.md with everything inlined makes that undiagnosable.
- [Keep skill.md Under 200 Lines and Use Progressive Disclosure](skills/skill-md-under-200-lines-progressive-disclosure.md): skill.md is a routing table, not a knowledge dump. Reference files load only when a step needs them, keeping context lean.
- [Make Your Skills Ask Through AskUserQuestion Instead of One Prose Block](skills/write-askuserquestion-into-skill-question-steps.md): A skill won't reach for the structured picker unless SKILL.md says so, so every question-gathering skill you own is silently degrading its own intake into a wall of text.
- [Name a skill `verify` and Claude runs it right before every code commit](skills/name-a-skill-verify-and-claude-runs-it-before-every-commit.md): A project or user skill called exactly `verify` makes Claude run it right before committing code, with no CLAUDE.md rule needed. It is an instruction to Claude, not an enforced block.
- [Name Several Skills in One Prompt and Let Them Compose](skills/stack-several-skills-in-one-prompt.md): Skills can be invoked together in a single request and nest up to five levels deep, so one prompt can produce a draft with one skill and route it through an editing skill on the way out.
- [Name the Skill in the Prompt When You Cannot Trust It to Auto-Invoke](skills/name-the-skill-explicitly-when-auto-invocation-is-unreliable.md): When a framework ships a skill that should fire for this step but does not reliably, say 'use the front-end UI engineering skill' by name instead of trusting the model to choose it.
- [On Pre-2.1.222 Versions, Check That a Gated Skill Isn't Being Reimplemented From Memory](skills/manual-only-skill-refusal-now-asks-you-to-run-it.md): Claude used to replicate a `disable-model-invocation` skill's workflow itself, defeating the gate while technically honouring it.
- [Prune a Skill's Learnings File on a Fixed Cadence or It Becomes Its Own Bloat Problem](skills/prune-skill-learnings-file-on-a-fixed-cadence.md): A per-skill learnings/rules file that accumulates feedback after every run needs regular pruning, on a fixed cadence such as weekly, or it grows into a second bloated document loaded on every invocation.
- [Put exact steps in skill scripts because only their output costs context](skills/skill-scripts-run-without-loading-their-source-into-context.md): Claude runs a skill's script through bash and does not read the script into context, so only the output uses tokens.
- [Put Skills in Nested .claude/skills Dirs in a Monorepo; Use dir:name on Clashes](skills/nest-skills-per-subproject-with-dir-qualified-names.md): Skills in nested .claude/skills dirs now load when you work on files there; on a name clash the nested skill is addressable as <dir>:<name>, so both coexist.
- [Put the install command next to each script in a skill so it works on a teammate's machine](skills/put-the-install-command-next-to-every-script-in-a-skill.md): Do not write 'use the PDF library'. Write 'install the required package' with the package name beside the script. If it is already installed Claude skips the step.
- [Put Your Design Principles in One Skill You Keep Updating, Not in Each New Prompt](skills/keep-your-design-principles-in-one-skill-you-keep-updating.md): Layering, scroll behaviour, motion and type principles belong in a single design skill the build invokes, so every site inherits them.
- [Qualify a claude.ai-Synced Skill as `anthropic-skills:<name>` in a Cloud Session](skills/prefix-claude-ai-synced-skills-with-anthropic-skills-in-cloud-sessions.md): the bare name resolves only when nothing else claims it.
- [Read a Near-Zero Plugin-Eval Delta as a Description Problem, Not a Skill-Body Problem](skills/a-near-zero-plugin-eval-delta-means-fix-the-skill-description.md): if the skill is never selected, its body is never read.
- [Rebuild Heavy-Use Skills From Scratch (Don't Edit) With a Stronger Model](skills/rebuild-heavy-use-skills-from-scratch-not-edit.md): Because models generate new code far better than they edit existing code, rebuild your most-used skills from scratch with a stronger model - in a clean folder from the original seed prompt so it can't copy the current skill - then diff old vs new and keep the better one.
- [Record Yourself Grading Output, Not Just Doing the Task](skills/record-an-evaluation-pass-to-build-a-cowork-evaluator-skill.md): Recording a task transfers process but not taste; narrate a bad example against a good one, turn it into a numbered checklist, and require the skill to cite pass/fail evidence on its own output.
- [Reverse-Engineer Skills From Successful Runs Instead of Designing Them Up Front](skills/reverse-engineer-skills-from-successful-runs.md): After Claude completes something well, ask it to turn that exact conversation, prompt flow, tool sequence, error handling, into a reusable skill; captures real decisions, not theory.
- [Run `claude plugin validate` on a Plain `.claude/skills` Directory - No Plugin Needed](skills/claude-plugin-validate-checks-a-bare-skills-directory.md): It now reports SKILL.md files whose frontmatter fails to parse, catching the entire class of "the skill exists but never loads.".
- [Run a Doc-Sync Skill That Reconciles Docs Against Your Diffs Before Committing](skills/doc-sync-pass-reconcile-docs-against-diffs.md): A doc-release skill diffs changed files, cross-checks every doc for contradictions, prunes dangling TODOs, and bumps the version before each commit.
- [Run a Read-Only Audit That Checks Every Routing and Index Claim Against What's Actually on Disk](skills/audit-knowledge-base-claims-against-disk-read-only.md): Routing tables and indexes are claims that drift silently; audit them against the filesystem red/yellow/green, and make the report state the concrete wrong answers you'd get today.
- [Run a Skill 'Cold' With a Vague Prompt to Confirm It Still Triggers and Does Not Collide](skills/run-a-skill-cold-to-verify-it-still-triggers.md): Test each skill by firing a deliberately vague prompt that does NOT name the skill, and check whether Claude still auto-invokes the intended one - if a sibling skill fires or none does, its name/description is not unique or trigger-heavy enough.
- [Run an Overnight Self-Improvement Loop on Your Skills](skills/skill-self-improvement-loop-binary-assertions.md): Give Claude an evals.json with 25 binary assertions; it loops autonomously, improve, commit, retest, until you stop it or it hits 100%.
- [Run the claude-api Skill's prompt-audit Subcommand Over Prompts Written for Older Models](skills/claude-api-prompt-audit-finds-old-model-prompt-patterns.md): Scaffolding that propped up weaker models now degrades newer ones, and it reads as thorough rather than harmful in review.
- [Scope Each Skill to a Single Step of the Process to Avoid Premature Completion](skills/scope-a-skill-to-one-step-of-the-process-not-the-whole-job.md): Models rush toward producing the asset. A skill that covers one step, such as research or outline or revision, resists that.
- [Set `background: false` on a `context: fork` Skill When You Need Its Result Inline](skills/set-background-false-on-forked-skills-you-need-inline.md): Forked-context skills now background by default, so a skill you relied on to answer into the current turn silently stops doing so.
- [Set each skill step's degree of freedom by what breaks if Claude does it differently](skills/set-skill-detail-per-step-by-how-fragile-the-step-is.md): Give plain goals where many approaches work, a template where a preferred shape exists, and an exact script where the step is fragile. One skill can mix all three.
- [Skills are interpreted differently by every model and harness, so retune them when you switch](skills/retune-skills-when-you-swap-model-or-harness.md): Rerun reference tasks after a model or harness swap and diff depth, sources, and tone against your accepted baseline.
- [Skills Should Be Complementary, Not Overlapping, One Skill Per Specific Goal](skills/skills-complementary-not-overlapping.md): Before creating a new skill, read all existing skill frontmatter for overlaps; a bug-hunting skill should call the DataDog skill rather than duplicating data fetching.
- [Skills Should Encode Unique Knowledge, Not Restate What Claude Already Knows](skills/skills-encode-lived-experience-not-obvious-behavior.md): A skill that restates obvious behavior pushes the distribution to a worse center; good skills move Claude to low-probability, high-value output.
- [Spend 15 Minutes Personalizing Any Marketplace Skill Right After Installing It](skills/personalize-a-single-installed-skill-in-15-minutes.md): Right after installing one marketplace skill, spend about 15 minutes adding a reference file with your brand voice and 3-5 example outputs you'd approve of, then add a one-line pointer in skill.md telling Claude to consult it before writing.
- [Split a multi-area skill's reference files by area so a question loads only one](skills/split-skill-reference-files-by-area-so-unrelated-ones-never-load.md): Give each domain its own reference file, linked from SKILL.md, so a revenue question never loads the marketing file.
- [Store a Skill's Persistent Log in the Claude Plugin Data Directory, Not the Skill Folder](skills/skill-log-in-claude-plugin-data-directory-survives-updates.md): A skill's continuity log/state file should be written to Anthropic's dedicated plugin-data storage rather than inside the skill's own folder, so it survives skill updates.
- [Strip Tools From the Model While a Skill Runs with `disallowed-tools` Frontmatter](skills/disallowed-tools-in-skill-frontmatter.md): Skills and slash commands can set `disallowed-tools` in frontmatter to remove specific tools from the model for the duration the skill is active, narrower than a global deny rule, scoped to just that workflow.
- [Teach Claude UI-Only Software With Cowork's Recorder Skill: Narrate While You Record, Then Answer Its Questions](skills/record-a-narrated-screen-demo-to-author-a-cowork-skill-for-ui-only-software.md): The Recorder Skill turns screen, clicks, and voice into a SKILL.md for software with no API or MCP; the narration carries the branching rules clicks can't show, and its question round catches defects in your own process.
- [Test a skill on every model you will run it with, asking one question per model](skills/test-a-skill-on-haiku-sonnet-and-opus-with-one-question-each.md): A skill's result depends on the model under it. Run it on each model you use and ask Haiku: enough guidance? Sonnet: clear and efficient? Opus: does it avoid over-explaining?.
- [The / Menu Lists Your Skills Ordered by How Often You Use Them](skills/slash-menu-orders-skills-by-usage-frequency.md): Opening the / menu shows your installed skills sorted by usage frequency, most-used first, rather than alphabetically or by install order.
- [The Gotchas Section Is the Most Valuable Part of Any Skill](skills/skill-gotchas-section-most-valuable-part.md): Encode failure modes and implicit knowledge as 'never do X' rules, this is how skills compound in value over time.
- [The Taste Skill Ships Named Sub-Skills (redesign, brutalist, minimalist): Invoke Them by Name to Get Different Aesthetics](skills/taste-skill-named-subskills-redesign-brutalist-minimalist.md): The taste skill is a collection, not one skill; asking Claude Code to 'use the redesign skill', 'reformat using the brutalist skill', or the minimalist skill on the same exported HTML produces genuinely different visual outcomes from the identical input file.
- [Turn an Example You Admire Into a Skill by Asking Claude Code to Analyze Why It Works](skills/turn-an-admired-example-into-a-skill-by-having-claude-analyze-why-it-works.md): Download a finished piece you like, ask Claude Code to explain why it is good, then ask it to turn that analysis into a reusable skill and apply the skill to your own brand.
- [Type a Skill's Name Directly to Run It, No Separate Command Needed](skills/type-a-skill-name-directly-to-run-it-as-a-slash-command.md): You can trigger any skill as a slash command just by typing its name (e.g. github-release-notes), without a separate wrapper command.
- [Use the Free Claude SEO and Claude Ads Skill Packs Instead of Building SEO/Ads Skills From Scratch](skills/claude-seo-and-claude-ads-third-party-skill-packs.md): A free, open-source third-party skill pack called Claude SEO offers 32 skills for technical SEO, content ranking, and AI/geo search audits, and a companion Claude Ads pack offers 33 skills across 12 ad platforms.
- [Use the Skill Creator's Eval System to Test and A/B Compare Skills](skills/skill-creator-ab-testing-and-evals.md): Run 5 parallel agents against binary assertions to score skill output; A/B test reference files before deciding what to keep.
- [Validate a Recorded Skill by Deleting the Demo Artifact and Re-Running It With Changed Inputs](skills/retest-a-recorded-skill-on-changed-inputs-not-the-demo-case.md): A recorded skill can pass by replaying what it watched; delete what the recording produced, change a key input, and prompt at goal level in a fresh chat to prove it generalized.
- [When a draft fails for a reason your style guide lacks, have Claude propose the rule and approve it](skills/have-a-skill-propose-a-new-checker-rule-when-a-draft-fails-for-a-missing-reason.md): At the end of a run, ask Claude to suggest a new rule for any failure the reference doc did not cover. You approve it, it goes into the doc, and later runs are checked against it.
- [When a Model Upgrade Breaks a Skill, Delete Instructions Rather Than Add Them](skills/simplify-a-skill-after-a-model-upgrade-instead-of-adding-detail.md): the examples that made a weaker model reliable now narrow a stronger one.
- [When Pruning Skills for a New Model, Delete the Procedure and Keep the Brand/Context Half](skills/keep-the-context-half-of-a-skill-delete-the-procedure-half.md): Boris Cherny's advice to delete your CLAUDE.md, skills, and hooks every ~6 months resolves, in practice, into a split: strip the step-by-step recipe (which caps a smarter model) but keep the parts the model cannot infer - where your files live, your header image, your brand colors, your links.
- [Write Skill Descriptions as Routing Logic, Not Marketing Copy](skills/skill-description-is-routing-logic-not-marketing.md): Community testing found skills activate only ~20% of the time with vague descriptions. List exact trigger keywords Claude would hear.
- [Write Skills Goal-First, Not as Rigid Recipes](skills/skill-goal-oriented-not-railroaded.md): Specifying every step collapses Claude's distribution to one narrow path; goal + context + gotchas lets Claude adapt.
- [Do Not Count on a Skill's model: or effort: Pin When Claude Invokes It: Only a Typed /skill-name Applies It](skills/skill-model-and-effort-pins-are-ignored-when-claude-invokes-the-skill.md): An open issue (reproduced on 2.1.296) reports that `model:` and `effort:` in skill frontmatter apply when you type `/skill-name` but are ignored when Claude calls the same skill through the Skill tool.
- [Start a mod build by telling Claude to load the plugin-authoring skill and re-read the docs](skills/tell-claude-to-load-the-plugin-authoring-skill-before-building-a-mod.md): Open the build prompt for a Claude Code mod with an instruction to load the plugin authoring skill so it primes itself on how mods work before writing code.
- [Audit Your Skill Library for Routing Collisions Where Two Skills Match the Same Request](skills/audit-your-skills-for-routing-collisions.md): Austin warns that when two skills could trigger on near-identical phrases (his example: 'What should I post?' and 'What should we post this month?'), neither pointing to the other, the wrong one fires.
- [Create Skills Task-First: Do the Task, Ask for a Skill, Run It, Then Correct and Enhance It](skills/create-skills-from-a-finished-task-then-run-and-correct-them.md): Austin's four steps: do the task, tell the AI to create a skill from that conversation, run the skill, and if the output is wrong correct it and say 'Based on this conversation, enhance the skill.'.
- [Have Your Interview Skill Build a Shared Domain Vocabulary So Code, Database, UI and Client Use the Same Terms](skills/interview-skill-that-builds-a-shared-domain-vocabulary.md): Matt Pocock uses a variant of his Grill Me skill, Grill with Docs, that builds a ubiquitous language (a domain-driven design idea) while it interviews you.
- [Install Only Stripe's best-practices Skill Rather Than the Whole Plugin, and Test Supabase as One Read-Only User](skills/install-only-the-vendor-skill-you-need-not-the-whole-vendor-plugin.md): Nate Herk warns that Stripe's full plugin might route every vendor decision through Stripe, so take only the stripe best practices skill, and set the Supabase connector read-only.
- [Keep a Skill Only If It Runs Exact Code, Knows What the Model Cannot, or Covers an API With No Connector](skills/three-question-test-for-whether-a-skill-still-earns-its-place.md): Ask three questions of every skill: does it run the same code each time, does it hold knowledge the model lacks and cannot look up, and is it still not replaced by a connector or MCP server.
- [Park Seasonal Skills Outside the Skills Folder and Have Claude Read Them Just in Time](skills/park-seasonal-skills-in-a-folder-outside-skills-and-read-them-on-demand.md): A skill is a cheat sheet, so skills you only need in one season can sit in a separate folder that is never loaded, and you ask Claude to read and follow that file when needed.
- [When Folding a Subagent Back Into the Main Agent, Make Its Prompt a Skill and Its Approval Gates Tools](skills/turn-subagent-prompts-into-skills-and-hard-limits-into-tools.md): A refunds or shipping subagent becomes a refunds or shipping skill (split into small referenced files), its API calls become tools, and any hard limit such as approval moves into the tool layer.
- [When an Artifact Format Works, Ask Claude to Turn It Into a Skill That Outputs That Template](skills/turn-a-liked-artifact-layout-into-a-skill.md): Austin iterated an artifact until it looked like his Instagram graphics, then asked for a skill called IG graphic creation whose artifact output is that format.
- [Switch Off the Built-In Skills You Never Trigger: They Load Into Every Session Regardless](skills/switch-off-built-in-skills-you-never-trigger.md): Built-in skills cost context in every session whether or not they fire, so audit the list and turn off the ones you never trigger (the author names the API reference skill firing on the word "Claude", Deep Research spawning helper agents, and the Artifact skills for a panel he had already blocked).

### Plugins

- [Add a Plugin Marketplace From the Terminal CLI: the Desktop App and VS Code Extension Can't](plugins/add-plugin-marketplaces-from-the-terminal.md): Adding a plugin marketplace only works from the Claude Code terminal CLI; the desktop app and IDE extension can't add one, though installed plugins then work everywhere.
- [Audit Plugins for force-for-plugin Output Styles: One Can Override Your outputStyle Setting](plugins/audit-plugins-for-force-for-plugin-output-styles-one-can.md): A plugin can ship an output style with force-for-plugin: true that applies the moment the plugin is enabled and overrides your own outputStyle, with load order deciding the winner when several do it.
- [Browse and Install Plugins from the Official Marketplace](plugins/plugin-system-install-from-marketplace.md): `/plugin` opens the official marketplace; plugins bundle skills + commands + hooks + MCP servers into one installable package.
- [Disable Auto-Update on a Plugin Marketplace to Pin a Version](plugins/disable-auto-update-on-a-plugin-marketplace.md): You can disable automatic updating for a specific plugin marketplace to keep an older, known-good version of it pinned on your machine.
- [Install a Workflow-Enforcing Plugin (e.g. Superpowers) to Force Plan → Build → Verify](plugins/superpowers-plugin-enforces-phased-workflow.md): Install a plugin like Jesse Vincent's Superpowers that orchestrates brainstorm → design → plan → TDD → verify so Claude can't skip discovery and jump to code.
- [Run /reload plugins After Installing a Repo-Local Plugin, or It Stays Inactive](plugins/reload-plugins-after-a-repo-local-plugin-install.md): Installing a plugin scoped to just the current repo writes to settings.local.json but doesn't activate it; you have to run /reload plugins (or restart) before Claude Code will use it.
- [Run the Compound Engineering Plugin for Diff-Conditional Review Lenses and a Solved-Bug Learning Loop](plugins/compound-engineering-plugin-diff-conditional-review-lenses-and-learning-loop.md): Every's Compound Engineering plugin is a 6-skill pipeline (ideate -> brainstorm -> plan -> work -> review -> compound) whose review step fires only the reviewer personas relevant to what your diff touched (design lens on UI, security on auth, Rails/Python/TS/DB reviewers by stack - 17 conditional plus always-on correctness/testing/maintainability) and whose compound step writes each solved problem to solutions/best-practices/*.md with 'applies when' metadata so future related work auto-pulls the fix.
- [Start From Anthropic's First-Party Skill Plugins Rather Than Trawling a Marketplace of Thousands](plugins/prefer-anthropics-first-party-skill-plugins-over-marketplace-trawling.md): Before searching skills.sh, install the vetted first-party packs from the plugins browser, for example the small business plugin's 31 ready-made skills, and adapt those rather than evaluating a marketplace one skill at a time.
- [Install Claude Code mods as plugins, and choose user or project scope](plugins/build-claude-code-mods-as-plugins-installed-at-user-scope.md): Mods (panes, footer bars, slash commands that add behavior) ship inside plugins.
- [Verify a model-router mod took effect with a flight-recorder mod before trusting the savings](plugins/pair-a-flight-recorder-mod-with-a-model-router-mod-to-verify-subagent-models.md): Mark runs a router mod that forces cheaper models for subagents and a flight-recorder mod that shows each request's model and tools, so the router's effect is visible.
- [Ask Claude to Save a Mod as a Plugin or It Is Temporary](plugins/ask-claude-to-save-a-mod-as-a-plugin-or-it-stays-temporary.md): Tristen says mods Claude builds are temporary by default; to keep one, ask Claude to save it as a plugin and install it.
- [Ask Claude to read your session logs and propose the top five mods to build](plugins/ask-claude-to-suggest-mods-from-your-session-logs.md): Mods ship inside plugins and are built in natural language, so have Claude mine your own session history for repeated phrases and tasks and name the five best mods for your workflow.
- [Build a mod that shows the prompt-cache countdown and offers handoff before it expires](plugins/build-a-cache-countdown-mod-with-one-click-handoff.md): A desktop mod can show the cache timer, context size and what a cold cache would cost, warn when the cache is about to go cold, and run your handoff skill plus /clear in two clicks.
- [Add a collision guard mod that asks before an edit to a file another chat touched in the last 30 minutes](plugins/build-a-collision-guard-mod-that-asks-before-editing-a-recently-touched-file.md): Before every Edit, Write or NotebookEdit, a mod checks whether the file was touched in the last 30 minutes and asks you to proceed, move to a worktree, or cancel, instead of silently overwriting a parallel session's work.
- [Add a record-mode mod toggled by a slash command to mask emails and figures while screen recording](plugins/build-a-record-mode-mod-that-masks-sensitive-values-on-screen.md): A mod can swap real emails and financial figures for placeholders in the displayed chat when you turn on a recording toggle, while Claude still reads the real text.
- [Use the Remotion Plugin in Claude Code When You Lack Claude Motion](plugins/remotion-plugin-in-claude-code-as-the-code-based-video-fallback.md): Tristen O'Brien says Claude Motion (prompt to editable animation) is only on Team and Enterprise plans in beta.
- [Build Mods on a Classifier-Gated Fork: A Small Model Decides, a Prompt-Cache-Sharing Fork Does the Side Work](plugins/gate-a-fork-behind-a-small-model-classifier-after-every-turn.md): The common pattern behind the useful Claude Code mods is: after each turn a small model (Haiku) answers a quick yes or no, and only on yes does a fork of the conversation run on the same prompt cache while the main session keeps working.
- [Get an Auto-Generated Diagram of What a Long Run Did With a Diagram-Pane Mod](plugins/auto-diagram-pane-mod-after-a-long-agent-run.md): After a big task, a Haiku check decides whether a lot of work happened, and if so a fork designs a diagram of the session's steps and decisions in a side pane.
- [Register a request-secret Tool in a Mod So Keys Go Straight to a Disk File Claude Cannot Read](plugins/secret-request-tool-mod-keeps-env-values-out-of-the-session.md): A mod can register a request_secret tool: when Claude needs a key it opens a pane, you type the value there, it is saved to a protected env folder, and Claude only ever sees a redacted placeholder, so the real value never lands in the transcript.
- [When You See a Feature Write-Up Online, Ask Claude to Turn It Into a Mod](plugins/turn-a-feature-you-read-about-into-a-mod-by-pasting-the-link.md): Paste the article or post and say 'turn this into a mod', and keep the result as your own plugin instead of waiting for the feature to ship.
- [Describe an Auto-Model Mod to Route Simple Prompts to Haiku and Builds to Opus, Then Check What It Costs You in Cache](plugins/build-an-auto-model-mod-that-picks-the-model-per-prompt-and-weigh-the-cache-cost.md): Tristen O'Brien built a mod that reads each prompt and switches the model, shown in the status bar: 'how many unread emails' went to Haiku, 'build a weather dashboard' to Opus.
- [Describe a Prompt-Upgrade Mod That Asks 3 Questions, Shows Before and After, and Runs the Result](plugins/build-a-prompt-upgrade-mod-that-interviews-you-and-rewrites-a-short-prompt.md): Tristen O'Brien's mod intercepts a short prompt, asks a few multiple-choice questions, shows your text beside a fuller rewrite you can edit, copy or run.
- [Re-Test Each Workflow Plugin Against Plain Claude Code With a Clear Brief, and Uninstall the Ones That Do Not Win](plugins/ab-test-a-workflow-plugin-against-no-plugin-with-a-clear-brief.md): Plugins built for models that skipped steps may add tokens without improving Opus 5.5 output. Run your usual prompts with a well-formed brief and no plugin, compare against the plugin output, and drop the plugin if there is no difference.

### Settings

- [A New Cost Field Was Added to Statusline Input](settings/statusline-cost-field-added.md): Claude Code's statusline input now includes a cost/currency usage field, so a custom statusline can display an accurate running cost, not just token or context counts.
- [Add a Privacy 'Quiet Mode' Env Bundle to settings.json for Client/Confidential Work](settings/quiet-mode-env-vars-for-client-and-confidential-work.md): For client or confidential projects, disable Claude Code's outbound analytics/telemetry, Sentry error reporting, the periodic feedback survey, and non-essential model calls via the settings.json env block, and untick 'Help improve AI models' in claude.ai privacy settings.
- [After Upgrading, Set OTEL_LOG_ASSISTANT_RESPONSES=0 or Telemetry Starts Logging Response Text](settings/otel-logs-assistant-responses-on-upgrade.md): The new assistant_response OTEL event follows OTEL_LOG_USER_PROMPTS, so if you already log prompts you start shipping full model output to your logs on upgrade, set it to 0 to stay prompts-only. (2.1.193).
- [Cap Bash Memory With `CLAUDE_CODE_TOOL_MEMORY_LIMIT` on Linux](settings/cap-bash-memory-with-tool-memory-limit-on-linux.md): Put Bash commands in a memory cgroup so a runaway build gets killed instead of taking the session and its in-flight work down.
- [Claude Code's Status Line Shows a Clickable Link to the Branch's Open PR](settings/claude-code-statusline-shows-clickable-active-pr-link.md): If you're on a branch with an open PR and have the GitHub CLI installed, Claude Code's status line displays a link you can click to open that PR directly.
- [CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC Also Switches Off Projects, Claude Design and claude.ai Plugin Downloads](settings/disable-nonessential-traffic-also-turns-off-projects-and-claude-design.md): `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC` also refuses Projects, Claude Design, live preview, claude.ai plugin downloads and /feedback, so set `DISABLE_TELEMETRY` alone if telemetry is all you want off.
- [CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL Is Now Only a Default: an Agent's model: Frontmatter Beats It](settings/claude-code-subagent-model-is-now-a-default-not-an-override.md): Since 2.1.251 any agent file's model: line or per-spawn override quietly escapes the env-var cost cap; audit .claude/agents/*.md and verify with /tasks.
- [Delete DISABLE_TELEMETRY and CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC to Turn Them Off: Setting Them to 0 Still Counts](settings/unset-telemetry-opt-out-vars-because-0-still-counts-as-set.md): Setting `DISABLE_TELEMETRY` or `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC` to 0 still counts as set, so delete the key to switch either back on.
- [Disable respondToBashCommands if You Use ! Just to Load Context Silently](settings/disable-respond-to-bash-commands-for-silent-context-loading.md): `!` bash commands now make Claude respond to the output automatically; set respondToBashCommands:false to keep ! as a silent context loader and avoid wasting a turn.
- [Dynamic Workflows Default to "Small" on Pro Plans Now, and "Medium" Dropped From 15 to 10 Agents](settings/workflow-size-medium-now-means-under-10-agents.md): The library's existing lesson (sourced from 2.1.219) that dynamic workflows default to "medium" (aim under 15 agents) is now out of date twice over: Pro plans default to "small," and "medium" itself was cut from under-15 to under-10 agents.
- [Feed the New Context-Remaining Statusline Variables to the /statusline Subagent](settings/feed-new-statusline-context-variables-to-statusline-subagent.md): Anthropic added two new template variables that give the statusline a more accurate read on remaining context; paste them into a prompt to the /statusline subagent and ask it to wire your statusline's context/battery indicator to use them directly instead of computing the estimate itself.
- [Move CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR and TMPDIR Out of Project settings.json env: They Are Ignored There Now](settings/config-dir-and-tmpdir-no-longer-set-from-project-settings-env.md): 2.1.251 stops project env blocks from setting CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR, CLAUDE_CODE_TMPDIR, and TMPDIR/TMP/TEMP, silently; move them to shell, user, or managed settings.
- [Put telemetry and OTEL variables in user or managed settings, project settings are now ignored for them](settings/telemetry-variables-in-project-settings-are-ignored-set-them-in-user-settings.md): A repo's `.claude/settings.json` can no longer switch on telemetry export or content capture, so a team dashboard setup committed to the repo stops working.
- [Replace Claude Code's @ Picker via the fileSuggestion Setting So It Ranks Symbols and Recency](settings/replace-the-at-file-picker-with-a-filesuggestion-command.md): A fileSuggestion command receives {"query": ...} on stdin per keystroke and prints repo-relative paths, so @ can resolve function names and rank exact basenames and recently-touched files first.
- [Set `CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_TODO_TOOLS=1` or Your Task-Tracking Workflow Silently Disappears](settings/todo-and-task-tools-removed-on-modern-models.md): TodoWrite and the whole Task tool family were removed from every current model in 2.1.233, turning any CLAUDE.md rule or `.claude/tasks/` pipeline that used them into a no-op.
- [Set `language` in Claude Code's Settings for Reliable Non-English Responses](settings/language-setting-for-reliable-non-english-responses.md): Adding a language parameter (e.g. 'Japanese') to Claude Code's settings file makes it reliably respond in that language, rather than depending on the model catching a hint from your prompt.
- [Set Any Setting Inline With /config key=value (Works in -p and Remote Control)](settings/set-settings-inline-with-config-key-value.md): `/config key=value` changes any setting straight from the prompt and works in headless (-p) and Remote Control sessions, where the interactive menu doesn't.
- [Set CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC=1 Instead of Listing Every Opt-Out Key](settings/flip-all-outbound-traffic-off-with-claude-code-disable-nonessential-traffic.md): Rather than setting DISABLE_TELEMETRY, DISABLE_ERROR_REPORTING and the rest one by one, CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC=1 turns the whole non-essential outbound bundle off with one env key.
- [Set CLAUDE_CODE_THRIFTY_SONIC=0 to Turn Off Auto Mode's Bash-First Editing Directive](settings/set-claude-code-thrifty-sonic-0-to-disable-bash-first-editing.md): Auto mode injects a harness directive steering Claude to read with cat/sed and edit with sed and heredocs; setting CLAUDE_CODE_THRIFTY_SONIC=0 in a settings.json env block turns that directive off so Claude goes back to the native Read, Edit, and Write tools.
- [Set the auto-compact window to a fixed token count: the percentage threshold is gone](settings/set-a-fixed-token-auto-compact-window.md): Unset, compaction now waits for the model's hard context limit, so pin CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_COMPACT_WINDOW to e.g. 100K before quality degrades.
- [Switch connector tool access from auto-load to "load tools when needed"](settings/set-connector-tool-access-to-load-tools-when-needed.md): The default loads every connector's full tool definitions into context on every message; the toggle defers them until actually used.
- [Sync of claude.ai Account Skills/Plugins Into Terminal Sessions Can Be Turned Off Per-Kind](settings/opt-out-of-claude-ai-skill-and-plugin-sync.md): As of 2.1.275, signing into a terminal Claude Code session with your claude.ai account automatically pulls in whatever skills and plugins are enabled on that account; two separate settings keys let you opt out of skills syncing, plugin syncing, or both independently.
- [The Claude Desktop App's Built-In Status Bar Already Shows Model, Effort, and Context Percentage](settings/desktop-app-status-bar-shows-model-effort-context.md): The Claude desktop app has a status bar (right side) that natively displays the current model, effort level, and context-window percentage, no custom statusline setup required.
- [Turn Off Auto-Filled Prompt Suggestions via /config](settings/disable-prompt-suggestions-in-config.md): Claude Code can proactively suggest your next prompt (e.g. suggesting 'push the changes' right after a commit) and let you Tab-autofill it; turn this off via /config if you find it distracting while coding.
- [Turn off WebFetch entirely with `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_WEB_FETCH`](settings/turn-off-webfetch-with-claude-code-disable-web-fetch.md): One env var removes WebFetch without writing a deny rule.
- [Set CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_ATTACHMENTS in Shell, User or Managed Settings: a Repo Settings File Can No Longer Set It](settings/set-claude-code-disable-attachments-in-user-or-shell-settings.md): Since 2.1.290 a repository's `.claude/settings.json` or `.claude/settings.local.json` cannot set `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_ATTACHMENTS`; only the shell, user settings and managed settings can.
- [Set CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_STRUCTURED_OUTPUTS When Titles, Memory Recall or Prompt Hooks Fail Behind a Gateway](settings/set-claude-code-disable-structured-outputs-behind-gateways-that-reject-them.md): Gateways that reject structured outputs used to break session titles, memory recall and prompt hooks; since 2.1.288 `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_STRUCTURED_OUTPUTS` turns structured outputs off.
- [Set CLAUDE_CODE_ATTRIBUTION_HEADER=0 When Your Gateway Reshapes the System Prompt: Omit the Block at the Client Rather Than Stripping It in the Gateway](settings/set-claude-code-attribution-header-0-when-your-gateway-reshapes-the-system-prompt.md): With `api.anthropic.com` upstream, the attribution block stays out of the prompt and cache key only when the gateway forwards `system` unchanged; a gateway that reorders, merges or stringifies it needs the variable set to `0`, and since 2.1.181 a pass-through gateway no longer needs it for caching.
- [Stop Restarting Research Sessions for WebSearch: the Budget Now Refills at 100 Calls an Hour](settings/websearch-budget-now-refills-hourly-instead-of-ending-at-200.md): Since 2.1.290 the interactive WebSearch budget refills over time (100 calls an hour) instead of ending after 200 calls; `CLAUDE_CODE_WEB_SEARCH_REFILLS_PER_HOUR` sets the rate and 0 turns refills off.
- [Pin Every Workflow Agent to One Model With CLAUDE_CODE_WORKFLOW_SUBAGENT_MODEL](settings/pin-workflow-agents-with-claude-code-workflow-subagent-model.md): Since 2.1.296 `CLAUDE_CODE_WORKFLOW_SUBAGENT_MODEL` runs every workflow agent on one model while other subagents keep theirs.
- [Lengthen the 529 Overloaded Backoff for Heavy Parallel Runs](settings/lengthen-the-529-overloaded-backoff-for-parallel-runs.md): `CLAUDE_CODE_OVERLOADED_RETRY_BASE_DELAY_MS` (2.1.292) and `CLAUDE_CODE_OVERLOADED_RETRY_MAX_DELAY_MS` (2.1.296) set a longer base and maximum delay when retrying an overloaded (529) request.
- [Let Claude Code Wait Out a Usage Limit and Resume the Task With autoContinueAtUsageLimit](settings/autocontinue-at-usage-limit-resumes-the-task-after-reset.md): When a claude.ai usage limit stops a session, Claude Code can wait for the reset and continue the task by itself instead of leaving a half-finished job.
- [Explore Subagents Run on Your Main Model: Route Cheap Search to Your Own Haiku Agent](settings/explore-subagents-run-on-your-main-model-route-cheap-search-to-your-own-haiku-agent.md): The built-in Explore agent no longer runs on Haiku: it inherits your main session's model (capped at Opus), so on an Opus session every Explore fan-out bills at Opus rates, and `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL` alone does not move it.
- [Give CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_COMPACT_WINDOW a k or M Suffix Only on 2.1.304 or Later](settings/auto-compact-window-suffix-values-silently-set-100k-before-2-1-304.md): Before 2.1.304, `CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_COMPACT_WINDOW=200k` silently gave a 100K window; from 2.1.304 the variable accepts `k` and `M` suffixes like `--autocompact` and warns when a value is out of range or ignored.
- [Use the claude_code.safety_event OpenTelemetry Event to See What Stopped or Prompted Each Tool Call](settings/use-the-safety-event-otel-event-to-find-rules-worth-adding.md): Since 2.1.307 Claude Code emits one OpenTelemetry event for each tool call that a permission rule, auto mode, a safety check, a hook or the sandbox stopped, prompted or sent back.
- [Set CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_EFFORT So Subagents Stop Inheriting the Session's Effort](settings/set-claude-code-subagent-effort-so-subagents-stop-inheriting-session-effort.md): Since 2.1.316, CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_EFFORT sets the effort level subagents run at when nothing else names one, so a session at high or xhigh no longer pays that rate for every grep-and-read subagent.
- [Set CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_ULTRATHINK When the Word Appears in Your Own Prompts or Rule Files](settings/set-claude-code-disable-ultrathink-when-the-word-is-in-your-prompts.md): 2.1.316 added CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_ULTRATHINK, a kill switch for the ultrathink keyword, so prose that merely mentions the word stops raising the turn's effort.
- [Set CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_TASKS=1 If You Never Use Background Agents: Their Status Summaries Are Model Calls Your Cost Tools Cannot See](settings/disable-background-tasks-if-you-never-use-background-agents.md): The short background-task status summaries are their own model calls, they do not appear in transcript-based cost tools, and one env var removes them if you do not run background agents.

### Configuration

- [Bound `CLAUDE_CODE_RESUME_INTERRUPTED_TURN` With a Max Age So Stale Turns Do Not Resurrect](configuration/bound-auto-resume-with-resume-interrupted-turn-max-age-ms.md): a 6 hour default cutoff, overridable per run.
- [Build a "Body of Work" Document With Your Core Thesis and Foundational Opinions](configuration/body-of-work-document-recurring-ideas.md): Extract the recurring ideas and stances you repeat into one markdown file (thesis + 5–8 core opinions); reference it in all content generation to anchor output to your POV.
- [Build a Brand Voice Profile From Real Examples So Output Sounds Like You, Not AI](configuration/brand-voice-profile-reduces-ai-detection.md): Extract your actual voice through personality/stance questions plus 3–5 real writing samples; Claude generates content that sounds authentically yours.
- [Build a Portable Setup on Open Standards to Avoid Vendor Lock-In](configuration/portable-setup-via-open-standards.md): Lean on AGENTS.md, the skills format, and MCP/CLI so your config survives a pricing change or a switch away from Claude Code.
- [Cap Each File in a Shared Brand-Context Folder at 50-100 Lines](configuration/cap-shared-business-brain-files-at-50-100-lines.md): When building a shared 'business brain' folder (tone of voice, audience, ICP, positioning, standards) that every skill references, keep each individual file to roughly 50-100 lines rather than one large brand document.
- [Connect the Product Wiki and Add a Rule to Consult It: and Stop When It Contradicts You](configuration/wire-the-org-wiki-in-and-tell-claude-to-flag-contradictions.md): A connector only makes the wiki reachable; a CLAUDE.md rule makes exploring subagents read it, and the contradiction clause stops a silent wrong pick between wiki and your request.
- [Create a tokens.json Design-System File for Consistent Generated Visuals](configuration/visual-design-tokens-json-for-consistency.md): Extract your brand visual identity (colors, typography, logos) into a tokens.json config; point all visual-generation skills at it.
- [Create One Shared Brand Context Folder That Every Skill References](configuration/shared-brand-context-folder-for-all-skills.md): One source of truth for voice, ICP, and positioning means all skills sound like you, without pasting brand context into every prompt.
- [Default Every New Skill or Rule to Project Scope, Promote to Global Only Once Proven](configuration/default-new-assets-to-project-scope-promote-to-global-when-proven.md): Keep new skills, rules, and CLAUDE.md additions scoped to the project they were built for by default ("everything is project until it deserves to be promoted to global").
- [Document the folder layout in CLAUDE.md so Claude files each output in the right place](configuration/document-your-folder-layout-in-claude-md-so-claude-files-outputs-correctly.md): A short folder map in CLAUDE.md let Claude reorganize a messy project in one shot, putting generated proposals, sites and decks under output/.
- [Fold Every Manual Brand-Voice Correction Back Into the Voice Profile File](configuration/update-brand-voice-file-on-every-manual-correction.md): Whenever you find yourself manually adjusting Claude's output to match your actual style, update the brand-voice rule file with that correction immediately instead of just fixing the one output.
- [Give Each Open-Weight Model Its Own Launch Alias via OpenRouter, and Trial Models There Before Buying Hardware](configuration/one-launch-alias-per-open-weight-model-via-openrouter.md): One shell alias per model makes switching a command name rather than a config edit, and renting a model per-token beats downloading 70GB to find out it's wrong for your workflow.
- [Include a Validation Loop in CLAUDE.md: Tell Claude How to Know It's Done](configuration/validation-loop-in-claude-md-is-critical.md): Without a success criterion in CLAUDE.md, Claude either stops too early or never stops. Include test commands and acceptance criteria.
- [Install Skills Per-Project, Not Globally: Every Skill's Description Taxes Session Startup](configuration/scope-skills-per-project-not-globally-to-cut-startup-context.md): Each installed skill/plugin loads its name and description into context at session start (so Claude can decide whether to invoke it), so a large global library silently taxes every session, install experimental or rarely-used skills into the specific projects that need them and audit the startup cost.
- [Keep `attribution` in Object Form in Any settings.json Shared Across CLI Versions](configuration/keep-attribution-in-object-form-in-shared-settings.md): 2.1.281 added a single settings.json switch, `"attribution": false`, that suppresses all commit/PR attribution at once (not just the session URL sub-setting the library already covers); but writing it as a bare boolean breaks the whole settings file on older CLI versions, so any settings.json shared across machines/versions should use the object form instead.
- [Keep a Gitignored Personal CLAUDE.md for Machine-Specific Notes Like 'You're Running in WSL'](configuration/gitignored-personal-claude-md-for-machine-specific-notes.md): Alongside your committed root and project CLAUDE.md files, keep a personal-preferences file that stays only on your machine and is never committed, for environment facts like running under WSL.
- [Keep CLAUDE.md Under 200 Lines and Use the Four-Level Hierarchy](configuration/claude-md-hierarchy-keep-it-under-200-lines.md): CLAUDE.md is a table of contents, not an encyclopedia. Keep it ≤200 lines; put detailed rules in separate reference files it points to.
- [Keep One Shared Rules Directory and Symlink It Into Every Repo](configuration/symlink-one-shared-rules-directory-into-every-repo.md): The rules folder follows symlinks, so maintain a single rules directory above your projects and symlink it into each repo instead of copying the same standards into every one.
- [Keep the Root CLAUDE.md a Pure Routing Table, Push Project-Specific Instructions Into Nested CLAUDE.md Files](configuration/root-claude-md-as-pure-router-nested-for-specifics.md): In a large multi-folder AI-operating-system repo, treat the top-level CLAUDE.md purely as a table of contents (here's where wikis live, here's where skills live, here's where projects live) rather than a system prompt, and rely on nested CLAUDE.md files inside subfolders for project-specific behavior.
- [Keep Your Personal CLAUDE.md to a Two-Line Pointer at the Shared Team File](configuration/personal-claude-md-as-a-pointer-to-the-shared-one.md): Boris Cherny's own personal CLAUDE.md is only about two lines long and just points to the team's shared CLAUDE.md, rather than duplicating or layering personal content on top of it.
- [Never Manually Maintain Config Files, Ask Claude to Update Its Own Configuration](configuration/never-manually-maintain-config-let-claude-do-it.md): Paste the desired config behavior into Claude and ask it to find and update the relevant settings file; it knows its own schema.
- [Park Notes-to-Self in CLAUDE.md HTML Comments, Not in Headed Sections](configuration/park-human-only-notes-in-claude-md-html-comments.md): Anything in CLAUDE.md that exists for you rather than for Claude belongs in an HTML comment block, which can span multiple lines.
- [Pin Your CLAUDE.md, Skills, and Hooks With claude plugin eval Cases Run on Every Release](configuration/pin-your-config-with-plugin-eval-cases-run-on-every-release.md): Config has no tests, so behaviour drifts silently across 25 releases a month; eval cases run with and without the setup loaded prove each rule still does work.
- [Point Claude Code at OpenRouter's free-models router to keep doing light knowledge work after you hit your limits](configuration/use-openrouters-free-models-router-as-a-rate-limit-fallback.md): Individual free models rotate, the router picks whichever is live; fine for slow light knowledge work, never for sensitive data.
- [Promote Any Fact You've Typed Into Chat Twice Into a Referenced File](configuration/typed-twice-rule-for-promoting-to-a-context-file.md): A concrete threshold for deciding what belongs in a context file: if you've typed the same piece of information into a Claude chat more than twice, turn it into a file and reference it from CLAUDE.md instead.
- [Put 'Remove All Mannered Prose' in Your Global Instructions to Fix Fable 5.1's Density](configuration/add-remove-all-mannered-prose-to-your-global-instructions.md): Fable 5.1 writes denser than Fable 5: longer sentences, fewer paragraph breaks.
- [Re-Verify Your No-Attribution Rule Actually Holds, It Was Being Overridden Before 2.1.269](configuration/the-attribution-reminder-no-longer-overrides-your-claude-md-rule.md): only managed settings can still force attribution now.
- [Route Claude Code to Cheaper or Local Models via Base-URL Settings When It Fits](configuration/route-claude-code-to-cheaper-or-local-models.md): Point Claude Code at OpenRouter or a local Ollama endpoint through base-URL/token settings to cut cost or run offline, best for bulk/execution work, not your hardest reasoning.
- [Run /design Inside the Project Folder and Tell It to Invent Nothing New](configuration/run-slash-design-inside-the-project-folder-so-it-reuses-your-tokens.md): /design reads the folder it is launched in for existing colours, fonts, logos and components.
- [Run Claude Code Inside Google's Free Antigravity IDE via Its Extension Marketplace](configuration/antigravity-hosts-claude-code-extension.md): Antigravity, Google's free desktop coding workspace, lets you search 'Claude' in its extensions panel and install Claude Code directly, and it also has its own free built-in agent builder as a fallback if you don't have a Claude subscription.
- [Select 'Local' Before Pressing Plan in the Claude Code Desktop App](configuration/desktop-app-plan-mode-select-local-target.md): The Claude Code desktop app now has a Plan button, but you must have 'local' selected as the target (versus a remote/cloud target) for it to plan against your actual local repository the way the CLI does.
- [Set `CLAUDE_CODE_WEBFETCH_DEADLINE_MS` When a Fetch Legitimately Needs Longer Than 5 Minutes](configuration/override-the-300-second-webfetch-deadline-when-a-fetch-must-wait.md): WebFetch now fails at 300 seconds by default.
- [Set attribution.sessionUrl to Keep claude.ai Session Links Out of Commits and PRs](configuration/omit-session-url-from-commits-with-attribution-setting.md): Web/Remote Control sessions stamp a claude.ai session link into commits and PRs; attribution.sessionUrl omits it, useful for public or mirrored repos.
- [Split the Brand Voice Profile Into a Core Voice File Plus Per-Platform Companion Guides](configuration/split-the-brand-voice-profile-per-platform.md): You do not have one voice, you have a different one per platform, so keep core voice DNA and rules in one file and generate separate LinkedIn, email and community guides by having Claude scrape what you have actually written on each.
- [Switch to the Built-in Concise Output Style Instead of Fighting Preamble in Every Prompt](configuration/switch-to-the-built-in-concise-output-style-instead-of.md): There is now a first-party Concise output style that strips preamble and narration without reducing engineering thoroughness or truncating error/security output.
- [Tag `@claude` on Coworkers' PRs to Fold Review Feedback Into CLAUDE.md](configuration/tag-claude-on-prs-to-update-claude-md.md): During code review, tag `@claude` on a PR (via the Claude Code GitHub Action, installed with `/install-github-action`) to add the lesson from your review comment to CLAUDE.md as part of that PR.
- [Tag Every Workaround in CLAUDE.md and Skills With a Revisit/Expiration Condition](configuration/tag-workarounds-with-revisit-expiration-condition.md): Add a 'revisit if / expires when' tag to every workaround you bake into CLAUDE.md and skills, so stale hacks get re-evaluated on the stated trigger instead of silently persisting long after the underlying problem is gone.
- [Turn On 'Show Last Response in External Editor' So Ctrl+G Carries Claude's Answer Too](configuration/show-last-response-in-external-editor.md): By default Ctrl+G opens only your own prompt in the external editor.
- [Use Context7 MCP to Pull Live Documentation Into Your Session](configuration/context-7-mcp-for-up-to-date-docs.md): Context7 fetches current library documentation into the context, preventing the AI from using outdated APIs that changed between training and now.
- [Use Google Workspace CLI to Give Claude Full Google Ecosystem Access](configuration/google-workspace-cli-for-native-google-integration.md): Google's open-source CLI gives Claude access to Drive, Gmail, Calendar, Docs, Sheets, and Slides via bash, properly formatted, not raw markdown.
- [Use Shared External Context Folders for a Team OS to Decouple Knowledge From Interface](configuration/team-agentic-os-shared-context-folders.md): Store editable team knowledge (procedures, templates, brand guidelines) in Notion or Google Drive, not Claude Code's native storage, resilient to tool changes and editable by non-technical teammates.
- [Use the Supabase MCP Server to Sync Your Database Schema Without Manual Queries](configuration/supabase-mcp-for-backend-sync.md): With Supabase MCP connected, Claude can query schema, inspect tables, and sync backend to code with a few prompts, read-only key recommended.
- [Vibe-Test a Shared CLAUDE.md Rule for Weeks Before Landing It on the Team](configuration/vibe-test-a-new-team-claude-md-rule-before-landing.md): Before committing a team-wide CLAUDE.md rule to the repo, run it privately for a few weeks and gather informal feedback, since there's no solved evaluation method for this yet.
- [When a CLAUDE.md rule keeps being ignored, shorten the file, don't shout the rule](configuration/when-a-claude-md-rule-is-ignored-shorten-the-file-not-the-emphasis.md): A disobeyed rule is a length symptom, not an emphasis problem; models follow ~150-200 instructions reliably.
- [When a Reply Reads as Jargon-Dense Word Salad, Branch It Into Several Rewrites and Save the One That Lands](configuration/branch-a-confusing-reply-to-author-a-custom-output-style.md): A/B candidate voices against a real jargon-dense reply on a branch, then turn the winner into a saved output style instead of guessing from the built-in list.
- [When Claude Code Makes a Mistake, Tell It to Fix the Narrowest Relevant CLAUDE.md, Not the Root One](configuration/route-claude-md-fixes-to-the-narrowest-scoped-file.md): After Claude Code errs, direct it to find and edit the most specific applicable CLAUDE.md file for that rule rather than defaulting to adding it at the root.
- [When rerouting Claude Code to another provider, replace the model in every env slot or subagents will spawn on a different model](configuration/swap-every-model-env-slot-when-rerouting-claude-code.md): Change only the main model and subagent spawns route to whatever is left in the other slots.
- [Turn On Claude in Chrome With --chrome, /chrome or User Settings: Project Settings Can No Longer Enable It](configuration/enable-claude-in-chrome-with-the-flag-or-user-settings-not-project-settings.md): Since 2.1.290 a project's settings files cannot turn Claude in Chrome on; use the `--chrome` flag, the `/chrome` command, or your user settings.
- [Point CLAUDE.md at Other Files by Backticked Path With a When-to-Open Line, Not an @-Import](configuration/reference-files-from-claude-md-by-path-with-a-when-to-open-line-not-at-import.md): Write 'read the client brief before any client work' with the path in backticks.
- [Give Claude Decision Frameworks to Bias Toward, Not Only Facts](configuration/give-claude-decision-frameworks-not-just-facts.md): State the bias Claude should lean on when choices are open, such as simplest best solution over most scalable, or clarity over creativity.
- [Prune CLAUDE.md Line by Line: Delete What Claude Already Does Unprompted, Keep What It Cannot See in Your Files](configuration/prune-claude-md-by-asking-would-removing-this-cause-mistakes.md): Simon Scrapes calls both 'add a line for every mistake' and 'delete the whole file every 6 months' half right. Test each line with 'would removing this cause Claude to make mistakes?', delete the ones it already follows, and keep decisions and preferences that are not visible in the repo.
- [Keep Each Nested CLAUDE.md Under the Size Limit: an Oversized One Now Loads Only Its Start](configuration/keep-nested-claude-md-under-the-size-limit-or-only-its-start-loads.md): Since 2.1.309 a nested CLAUDE.md over the size limit loads only its start (before, reading files under it could make a session compact repeatedly), so rules near the end of a huge per-folder file are dropped.
- [With Both CLAUDE.md and AGENTS.md in a Repo, Set Project Instructions to Load Both or AGENTS.md Is Skipped](configuration/set-project-instructions-to-load-both-claude-md-and-agents-md.md): The default Project instructions mode, `claude-md-or-agents-md`, loads AGENTS.md only where a project has no CLAUDE.md, so a repo with both silently ignores AGENTS.md; switch to `claude-md-and-agents-md` in /config or import `@AGENTS.md` from CLAUDE.md.

### Permissions

- [A broad `Bash` allow rule no longer silently covers writes to credential stores Claude's file tools refuse](permissions/broad-bash-allow-rules-now-prompt-for-credential-file-writes.md): Even `Bash` with no pattern in allow, or a hook that approves, now stops and asks before a shell command writes to the profile store or credentials file.
- [A Second Symlink-Write Permission Bypass Was Fixed in 2.1.280, Separate From the 2.1.251 Round](permissions/upgrade-past-2-1-280-for-the-symlink-write-fix.md): This library already has a lesson about symlink permission bypasses fixed in 2.1.251; 2.1.280 fixed a distinct one: a write through a symlinked path was being permission-checked against how the path is spelled inside the tree, not where the symlink actually resolves to, so acceptEdits/allow-rules/auto-mode could approve a write that actually landed outside the approved directory.
