- [Swap MCP connectors for API keys before you deploy an app Claude built](gotchas/swap-mcp-connectors-for-api-keys-before-deploying-an-app.md): MCP connectors work inside Claude Code only. A deployed site or app cannot use them, so its integrations need API keys.
- [Tell the agent to `curl` a URL: WebFetch summarizes instead of returning raw source](gotchas/tell-the-agent-to-curl-a-url-webfetch-summarizes-instead-of.md): When you want an agent to consume a page's actual source or full text (not a summary), say "use curl to fetch" in the prompt, because Claude Code's default WebFetch tool runs the page through a summarizing model first.
- [The # Shortcut for Adding a Note to CLAUDE.md Was Removed](gotchas/hashtag-shortcut-removed-for-claude-md.md): The `#` shortcut that used to let you quickly append a note to CLAUDE.md by typing `#` and a note has been removed.
- [The Claude Code desktop app overrides BYO-model env settings, route to other providers from the terminal or IDE](gotchas/desktop-app-ignores-byo-model-env-use-the-cli.md): Alternate-provider routing via the settings env block only works from the terminal or IDE; the desktop app forces a Claude model.
- [Upgrade WSL and Windows Claude Code Separately: They Are Two Installs on Two Update Tracks](gotchas/wsl-and-windows-claude-code-are-separate-installs.md): If you mirror a Claude Code setup across WSL and native Windows, the two are separate installs that update independently; check the Windows one explicitly with `claude --version` instead of assuming the WSL upgrade covered it.
- [Use a Hosted Whisper API Instead of Local whisper.cpp When Running Several Video Sessions at Once](gotchas/hosted-whisper-api-for-concurrent-video-transcription.md): Local whisper.cpp transcription for word-level video timestamps eats a lot of RAM; if you're running multiple video-editing agent sessions in parallel, switch to a hosted Whisper API (e.g. via an OpenAI key) instead.
- [Watch for the Agent-Team Leader Implementing Directly Instead of Delegating](gotchas/agent-team-leader-may-implement-instead-of-delegate.md): The orchestrator sometimes starts writing code itself rather than assigning it to a teammate, or starts a new task before waiting for teammates to finish their current one.
- [When the desktop Files panel misrenders a dragged-in folder, ask Claude to inventory it: trust the read, not the panel](gotchas/verify-dragged-in-assets-by-asking-claude-to-inventory-them.md): The panel is a display layer, not ground truth; trust the model's directory read.
- [With the Sandbox On, `git add .` Fails Outright and `git status` Shows Phantom Files](gotchas/sandbox-mounts-break-git-add-dot-and-fake-untracked-files.md): Protected-path stubs abort staging with exit 128 (taking legitimate files with them) and leave ~19 phantom untracked entries the model treats as real state.
- [Without Usage Credits, Switch Off the 1M-Context Model or Sessions Wedge](gotchas/avoid-the-1m-context-model-without-usage-credits.md): On plans without usage credits, defaulting to or switching to the 1M-context model fails with 'Usage credits required for 1M context' and can wedge mid auto-compaction, pick the standard-context (non-[1m]) model.
- [Your Account Email Is in Claude Code's System Prompt - Redact Before Sharing a Transcript](gotchas/your-account-email-is-in-the-system-prompt-every-session.md): A `# userEmail` block is injected every session from `~/.claude.json`, and `IS_DEMO=1` masks it in output but does not remove it from the system prompt.
- [Press Up on the Empty Prompt to Recover a Draft You Cleared With Ctrl+C](gotchas/press-up-to-recover-a-prompt-cleared-with-ctrl-c.md): A long prompt wiped by Ctrl+C is not lost: pressing Up on the empty prompt brings the draft back, pasted text and images included.
- [Ask Claude to Keep Reading a Long Page With WebFetch offset, and Distrust Long Fetches From Before 2.1.290](gotchas/webfetch-reads-long-pages-in-chunks-ask-for-the-rest-with-offset.md): WebFetch used to drop page text past 100,000 characters without saying so.
- [Write CLAUDE_CODE_USER_DIALOG_TIMEOUT_MS as Plain Milliseconds, Not a Value With a Unit](gotchas/give-claude-code-user-dialog-timeout-ms-a-plain-millisecond-value.md): A unit-suffixed value such as `5m` used to be read as 5 ms and cancel remote dialogs at once; since 2.1.290 it falls back to the `dialogExpiry` setting, so it still does not mean five minutes.
- [Path-Scoped Rules Now Load on Write, Edit and Single-File Bash Reads, but Still Not on Bash Edits](gotchas/path-scoped-rules-now-load-on-write-edit-and-bash-reads-but-not-bash-edits.md): Path-scoped `.claude/rules` and nested CLAUDE.md files used to load only when the Read tool touched a file; 2.1.288 added Write and Edit, and 2.1.293 added single-file `cat`, `head`, `tail`, `sed -n` and `grep` reads in Bash.
- [After EnterWorktree With path:, Read the Worktree's Root CLAUDE.md and AGENTS.md Yourself](gotchas/read-the-worktree-root-claude-md-yourself-after-enterworktree-with-path.md): An open issue reports that `EnterWorktree` with `path:` never loads the worktree's root CLAUDE.md or its `@AGENTS.md` import; only subdirectory CLAUDE.md files load.
- [Before Using a Claude Code Project, Check That Only Threads Get Connectors and That Threads Default to Opus on High Effort](gotchas/claude-code-projects-main-chat-has-no-connectors-and-threads-default-to-opus-high.md): In the Projects beta the main conversation cannot use your connectors, only its threads can, and threads default to Opus on high effort, which burns limits fast.
- [Don't Install RTK, Caveman or Ponytail on Their Advertised Savings: Measure Where Your Tokens Go](gotchas/check-token-saver-tools-against-independent-measurements-before-keeping-them.md): Nate Herk reports a JetBrains test where the advertised savings mostly did not hold, and suggests Codeburn to see real spend and waste.
- [Expect Write to Refuse Windows-1252, Shift-JIS, GBK and UTF-16BE Files Instead of Silently Converting Them to UTF-8](gotchas/write-now-refuses-legacy-encoded-files-instead-of-converting-them.md): Since 2.1.299 Write refuses legacy-encoded files it used to re-encode to UTF-8 without telling you, and Read handles UTF-16LE with a byte-order mark correctly.
- [On a Max or Team Seat, Check That Interactive Sessions Still Write the 1-Hour Cache, and Pin promptCacheTtl "1h" If They Do Not](gotchas/check-interactive-sessions-still-write-the-1-hour-cache-and-pin-promptcachettl.md): Reporters saw interactive sessions silently drop to 5-minute cache writes from 2026-10-12 while headless runs kept 1 hour, and setting `"promptCacheTtl": "1h"` restored 1-hour writes on the next turn.
- [Triage Adversarial-Review Findings Against Your Trust Boundaries Before the Agent Fixes Them All](gotchas/triage-adversarial-review-findings-against-trust-boundaries-before-fixing.md): An adversarial reviewer will push the fixer to add defenses against hypothetical threats, so a person has to decide which findings to reject before the agent applies them.
- [A Dim "Not run" Row in Auto Mode Means the Classifier Failed: Retry, Don't Write a Rule](gotchas/auto-mode-not-run-row-means-the-classifier-failed-not-a-denial.md): Since 2.1.296, a tool call that auto mode skipped because its check returned no usable answer shows as a dim `Not run · auto mode's check had no usable answer` row; that is a classifier or server failure, not a judgement about your command, so allow rules and environment entries cannot fix it.
- [Dedupe on message.id When Summing Tokens From Claude Code Transcripts](gotchas/dedupe-on-message-id-when-summing-tokens-from-claude-code-transcripts.md): Claude Code writes one JSONL line per content block of an assistant response (thinking, text, each tool call), and every one of those lines repeats the same `usage` object, so summing `usage` over the lines of `~/.claude/projects/<project>/*.jsonl` overcounts tokens 2 to 3x.
- [Make Your Gateway Forward cache_control Unchanged: One That Strips It Bills Every Turn Uncached, With No Error](gotchas/a-gateway-that-strips-cache-control-bills-every-turn-uncached.md): A gateway that removes Claude Code's `cache_control` markers returns success while billing the whole conversation uncached on every turn (flattening block-form system content to a string drops the marker the same way); one that rejects the mid-conversation block's marker with a `400` costs only that block.
- [With Telemetry or Nonessential Traffic Off, the Monitor Tool Only Exists From 2.1.310](gotchas/monitor-tool-now-works-with-telemetry-disabled-from-2-1-310.md): Before 2.1.310 the Monitor tool (and plugin monitors) was missing on Bedrock, Vertex and Foundry and in sessions with telemetry or nonessential traffic disabled; 2.1.310 adds it there and also blocks long foreground sleeps there.
- [A Shared Claude Slide-Deck Artifact Link Needs a Claude Account to Open](gotchas/shared-artifact-links-need-a-claude-account-to-open.md): Austin warns that when you send someone the link to a slide-deck artifact, they need a Claude account to open it.
- [Track Rounds-to-Pass on Agent Review Loops: One PR Ran More Than 50 Rounds Overnight](gotchas/track-rounds-to-pass-on-agent-review-loops.md): When an agent writes and an agent reviews, the loop can run unbounded: the Claude Code team saw one PR go through more than 50 review and fix rounds overnight, and a git blame on what the reviewer kept flagging found about 59% was code present since its first pass.
- [Agents Have Put Your Account Email in Outbound Request Headers: Update to 2.1.317 and Keep the Privacy Mod On](gotchas/expect-a-prompt-before-a-web-or-shell-call-sends-your-account-email.md): Claude Code puts your account email in the system prompt, and in one week several users caught agents copying it into the User-Agent header of fetches to sites like Wikipedia.
- [Measure the 5-Hour Window Before Moving Work to claude -p: Users Report It Drains 1.3x to 2.9x Faster](gotchas/measure-the-5-hour-window-before-moving-work-to-claude-p.md): Several users running controlled A/B tests report that the same work through `claude -p` (entrypoint sdk-cli) or the Agent SDK (sdk-ts) drains the 5-hour usage window much faster than the interactive TUI or the VS Code extension.
- [Check Which Registry Your IDE Pulls the Claude Code Extension From: a Trojanized Build Was Served From One Marketplace for Hours](gotchas/check-which-registry-your-editor-installs-the-claude-code-extension-from.md): A modified Claude Code extension build (2.1.313) that planted an ssh backdoor was served from one marketplace for an 11-hour window on 2026-10-29, which makes the registry your editor installs from part of your threat model.

### Hooks

- [Add `fork` to SessionStart Hook Matchers: Forked Sessions No Longer Report `resume`](hooks/add-fork-to-sessionstart-hook-matchers.md): A matcher written for `startup|resume|clear` now has a hole in it exactly where the new forked-session workflow lives.
- [Block the Real Run Until the Dry Run Has Happened, and Show Which Mode You Are In](hooks/gate-real-runs-behind-a-dry-run-with-hook-state.md): Because function hooks hold state across turns, a hook can refuse every real command until the dry-run command has actually been executed in that session, and paint a large marker in the UI saying whether you are in dry mode or production.
- [Branch SessionStart Resume Hooks on Session Staleness and Estimated Re-Cache Cost](hooks/sessionstart-resume-hooks-receive-staleness-and-recache-cost.md): Since 2.1.251, SessionStart hooks firing on resume receive the session's staleness and the estimated re-cache cost, so a resume hook can decide whether reinjecting context is worth it instead of always paying for it.
- [Cache Expensive Tool Results in the Function Hook Store and Short-Circuit the Repeat Call](hooks/cache-tool-results-in-the-function-hook-store.md): Function hooks come with a store.
- [Call a Cheap Model From Inside a Function Hook With $model](hooks/call-a-cheap-model-from-a-function-hook.md): Hooks can invoke a model.
- [Draw a Live Status Row in Claude Code From a Function Hook](hooks/draw-a-live-status-row-from-a-function-hook.md): Function hooks can add UI.
- [File and Index Conversation Data Silently via SessionEnd and PreCompact Hooks](hooks/silently-index-conversations-on-sessionend-and-precompact-hooks.md): MemPalace-style verbatim memory systems file and index every conversation in the background using hooks on session end and right before compaction, not just at session start.
- [Gate Mid-Session Model Switches With the New PreModelSwitch Hook](hooks/gate-model-switches-with-a-premodelswitch-hook.md): 2.1.251 makes model switches hookable (block, confirm, annotate), so a routing rule that lived in CLAUDE.md as a suggestion becomes enforceable, and resume hooks now see re-cache cost.
- [Guard a Skill's .env With Skill-Scoped Hooks and a Read-Only API Key](hooks/guard-skill-env-secrets-with-skill-scoped-hooks.md): When a skill's bundled scripts need an API credential, store a read-only key in the skill's own .env and add skill-scoped hooks that block Claude from reading the .env directly or making unapproved web requests; the scripts use the key, the model only sees their output.
- [Hook Into Agent-Team-Specific Lifecycle Events: Teammate-Idle and Task-Complete](hooks/agent-team-idle-and-task-complete-hooks.md): Beyond the standard Claude Code hooks, agent teams expose their own hook events for a teammate going idle and for a task completing, letting you trigger side effects on team lifecycle changes.
- [In an External Plan-Review Hook, Only Redo the Plan on an Explicit 'Block' Verdict](hooks/plan-review-hook-only-blocks-on-explicit-block-verdict.md): When an ExitPlanMode hook sends the plan to Codex CLI and Gemini CLI for review, structure the flow so a 'warning' verdict from either reviewer lets Claude proceed with execution, and only an explicit 'block' verdict makes it redo the plan.
- [Make a Hook Ask Whether to Split Any File It Is About to Push Past 1,000 Lines](hooks/ask-before-oversized-file-edits-with-a-hook.md): Models still write 2,000 and 3,000 line files.
- [Mirror Every Hook Event to Your Own Log Store for an Audit Trail](hooks/mirror-every-hook-event-to-your-own-log-store.md): In a regulated setting (healthcare, payments), register a function hook on every Claude Code event that ships the result to your organisation's log store, so there is an auditable record of exactly what the agent did.
- [Override a Built-In Tool With a Function Hook So You Can Uninstall Its MCP Server](hooks/override-a-built-in-tool-with-a-function-hook.md): Intercept WebSearch in a function hook, send the query to the Exa API when the key is present, and fall back to the built-in tool when the key is missing or the call fails.
- [PermissionRequest Hooks Can No Longer Be "agent" Type: Use command or http](hooks/permissionrequest-hooks-must-be-command-or-http.md): An agent-type hook (one that dispatches to a subagent) attached to the PermissionRequest event will no longer run at all as of 2.1.280; it now errors immediately instead of silently doing nothing useful, because an agent hand-back can't return the allow/deny decision that event needs.
- [Put Recurring Reminders in a SessionStart Hook With Day-of-Week Logic](hooks/sessionstart-hook-day-of-week-reminders.md): Branch on the weekday to surface your periodic maintenance skills in the terminal you actually work in; note the desktop app hides SessionStart output entirely.
- [Re-Inject a Curated Context File on the PreCompact Hook So Key Facts Survive Compaction](hooks/precompact-hook-reinjects-context-to-survive-compaction.md): Wire a PreCompact hook that injects a hand-maintained _context.md (identity, current goal, key decisions) into the session right before compaction, so the lossy auto-summary can't drop what matters.
- [Redact Secrets at the Prompt With a Function Hook That Swaps in an ID](hooks/redact-secrets-before-they-enter-the-transcript.md): A function hook can intercept your input, detect high-entropy strings, emails and IP addresses, replace each with an ID, hold the real value in the hook's in-memory store, and substitute it back only when a tool actually needs it.
- [Return Context Strings From a PreToolUse Hook to Brief the Model Before It Proceeds](hooks/pretooluse-hook-context-strings-brief-the-model.md): A PreToolUse hook is not only an allow/block gate: it can pass additional context strings back to the model, which reads them before proceeding with the tool call, for example injecting a memory index right before the action.
- [Rewrite a Tool Call's Input in a Function Hook Instead of Blocking and Re-Prompting](hooks/rewrite-tool-input-in-a-function-hook.md): Shell hooks can only allow or block.
- [Rewrite Single-Segment `dir/**` Hook Conditions as `**/dir/**`: They No Longer Match at Any Depth](hooks/rewrite-single-segment-hook-glob-conditions-for-any-depth.md): The hook still loads and validates, it just stops firing on nested paths, and `deny`/`ask` permission rules kept the old any-depth semantics, so the two surfaces now disagree.
- [Run `claude --init` to Auto-Trigger an Onboarding Hook for New Teammates](hooks/claude-init-flag-triggers-onboarding-hook.md): `claude --init` launches Claude Code normally but also fires a defined hook, letting you script onboarding steps (installing npm packages, starting local services like a database) that run automatically the first time someone sets up the project.
- [Scope PreToolUse/PostToolUse/Stop Hooks to One Subagent, Skill, or Command in Its Own Frontmatter](hooks/scope-hooks-to-one-subagent-skill-or-command-in-its-frontmatter.md): Subagents, skills, and slash commands can define their own pretooluse, posttooluse, and stop hooks directly in frontmatter, so the hook only fires for that agent, skill, or command instead of every task via settings.json.
- [Set `once: true` on a Hook So a Side-Effecting Command Fires Only Once](hooks/set-once-true-to-prevent-duplicate-hook-runs.md): A hook whose event keeps matching will re-run its command; adding `once: true` to the hook definition prevents duplicate runs, so a side-effecting hook like a plan-archiving script executes exactly once.
- [Start Claude Code With CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_FUNCTION_HOOKS=1 to Get the plugin-authoring Skill](hooks/enable-function-hooks-to-unlock-plugin-authoring.md): Function hooks sit behind an environment flag.
- [Use `claude --maintenance` With a Hook Dispatcher to Separate Init Logic From Maintenance Logic](hooks/claude-maintenance-flag-with-dispatcher-script.md): `claude --maintenance` triggers a distinct hook from `--init`/`--init-only`; build a small dispatcher script that checks which mode invoked the hook and branches to the matching sub-script (e.g. npm install for init, formatting/doc updates/dependency bumps for maintenance).
- [Use a UserPromptSubmit Hook to Auto-Inject Top Semantic Memory Matches Into Every Prompt](hooks/userpromptsubmit-hook-auto-injects-semantic-memory-matches.md): mem-search wires a UserPromptSubmit hook that, on every prompt you type, searches your memory files semantically and injects the top few matches into context automatically, without you asking Claude to search.
- [Verify SessionStart Hook Output in the Terminal CLI: the Desktop App Hides It](hooks/desktop-app-hides-sessionstart-hook-output.md): SessionStart hook messages only appear in terminal Claude Code; the desktop app hides the hook's output, so a session-open reminder silently does nothing there and you conclude the hook is broken.
- [Re-test Deny Rules Against the Output of Any PreToolUse Hook That Rewrites Tool Input](hooks/retest-deny-rules-against-what-a-rewriting-pretooluse-hook-produces.md): Before 2.1.290, some permission rules and safety checks were not applied to a tool call after a PreToolUse hook rewrote its input (`updatedInput`).
- [When a Tool Call Is Blocked and No Rule Explains It, Check the PreToolUse Hook Matcher First](hooks/a-guard-hook-that-cannot-match-or-serialize-now-blocks-the-call.md): Since 2.1.288, if matching a PreToolUse or PermissionRequest hook fails, or the tool input cannot be serialized to JSON, the call is blocked instead of the hook being skipped.
- [Set onFailure: "block" on Guard Hooks So a Hook That Cannot Run Stops the Action](hooks/set-onfailure-block-on-guard-hooks-so-they-fail-closed.md): By default a command or HTTP hook that cannot start, times out, or exits with an unexpected code lets the action through; since 2.1.295 `onFailure: "block"` makes it fail closed.
- [Re-test prompt and agent Hooks Written as Instructions: Before 2.1.294 They Could Allow What They Described Blocking](hooks/retest-prompt-and-agent-hooks-written-as-instructions.md): A `prompt` or `agent` hook phrased as an instruction ("Block commands that...") could approve the very thing it named until 2.1.294; Stop and SubagentStop prompt hooks phrased that way also stopped Claude early.
- [Find a Slow Hook With claude --debug: Finished Hooks Now Log Their Duration](hooks/find-a-slow-hook-with-claude-debug.md): Since 2.1.296 `--debug` logs the command, plugin, outcome and duration of command hooks on tool calls, prompts, SessionStart and Stop when they finish.
- [Add a Stop hook that blocks the answer if the agent wrote code and never ran it](hooks/add-a-stop-hook-that-blocks-finishing-when-code-was-written-but-not-run.md): Nate Herk's Karpathy agent includes a hook that fires when the agent tries to finish its turn and sends it back with one message if code was written but not run.
- [Attach a Prompt or Agent Hook to the Stop Event So a Second Model Checks the Work Before Claude Can Finish](hooks/attach-a-prompt-or-agent-hook-to-stop-so-a-second-model-checks-the-work.md): A hook no longer has to be a script: a prompt hook has a small model read the conversation, and an agent hook can open files, and either can send Claude back to work with a reason when the work is not acceptable.
- [Match UserPromptSubmit Hooks on the Typed Slash Command: They Now Run Before a Skill or Command Expands](hooks/userpromptsubmit-hooks-now-run-before-a-skill-or-command-expands.md): Since 2.1.299 a UserPromptSubmit hook sees the prompt before a skill or custom slash command expands, and a block now also stops the command's embedded shell lines.
- [On Windows, Install Git for Windows or Set a Hook's shell: a Guard Hook That Names No Shell Now Blocks Without It](hooks/on-windows-a-shell-less-guard-hook-now-blocks-without-git-for-windows.md): Before 2.1.299 a PreToolUse or PermissionRequest hook with no `shell` was silently skipped on a Windows machine without Git for Windows; now the failed guard denies the call.
